### [1] [A Lapse in the Cosmological Constant Problem with Bulk Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12460)
*Justin Khoury,Benjamin Muntz,Antonio Padilla*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文将对宇宙学常数问题的新方法从超局域z=0极限推广到包含z=1形变的动态框架，证实在背景方程层面真空能抵消仍然成立，但需要Fierz-Pauli关系来消除标量鬼影。该框架可协变地实现为五维爱因斯坦引力、顶形式通量和一个间隔类khoron标量场，然而环绕紧致维传播的物质会产生拓扑敏感的Casimir贡献，需要额外谱条件来抑制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前提出的基于紧致额外维中各向异性标度的方法仅在超局域z=0极限下有效，该极限下引力场方程对物质真空能的辐射修正不敏感。需要将该框架扩展到z=1形变，以恢复沿紧致方向的动力学，从而构建更完整且物理上可实现的宇宙学常数解决方案。

Method: 引入z=1形变以恢复紧致方向的动力学；分析背景方程中的真空能抵消机制；通过二次谱分析确定外曲率耦合需满足Fierz-Pauli关系以消除标量鬼影；提出协变实现：五维爱因斯坦引力加顶形式通量和间隔类khoron标量场，利用辅助einbein实现全局约束；分析物质环绕紧致维传播导致的Casimir效应。

Result: 真空能在背景方程层面仍然抵消；Fierz-Pauli关系使耦合谱无鬼影；协变公式中重整化物质真空能的常数平移从内禀爱因斯坦方程中代数消去；但环绕紧致维传播的物质产生有限的、拓扑敏感的Casimir贡献，无法自动抵消，需额外谱条件抑制。

Conclusion: 该框架成功超越了超局域极限，在保持真空能抵消的同时提供了健康的二次谱，并给出了协变的五维实现。然而，Casimir效应带来的非零贡献是机制无法自动处理的残留问题，必须通过附加的谱条件加以解决，这为后续研究指明了方向。

Abstract: We have previously proposed a new approach to the cosmological constant problem based on anisotropic scaling in a compact extra dimension. In the ultra-local $z=0$ limit, the interplay between a projectable lapse, foliation-preserving diffeomorphisms, and higher-form fluxes renders the gravitational field equations insensitive to radiative corrections to the matter vacuum energy. Here we extend this framework beyond the ultra-local limit by introducing $z=1$ deformations that restore dynamics along the compact direction. We show that vacuum-energy cancellation persists at the level of the background equations, while generic extrinsic-curvature couplings propagate an additional scalar ghost. A healthy quadratic spectrum selects the Fierz-Pauli relation between these couplings. This motivates a particularly simple realization in terms of five-dimensional Einstein gravity, a top-form flux, and a spacelike khoron scalar field that dynamically defines a preferred projectable foliation. In this covariant formulation, the global constraint arises from an auxiliary einbein on the space of khoron leaves, and constant shifts of the renormalized matter vacuum energy cancel algebraically from the intrinsic Einstein equations. Finally, allowing matter to propagate around the compact dimension generates finite, topology-sensitive Casimir contributions that are not automatically removed by the mechanism. Suppressing these contributions requires additional spectral conditions on the propagating bulk degrees of freedom.

</details>


### [2] [Stabilizer complexity and the Python's lunch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12472)
*Abhirup Bhattacharya,Jatin Narde,Onkar Parrikar,Suprakash Paul*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了全息CFT中部分纠缠热（PET）态约化密度矩阵的稳定子复杂度。作者发现Wigner负性（一种可用于衡量量子态制备经典模拟复杂度的魔幻单调量）由外极值面面积与最小极值面面积之差决定，即exp[(A_out - A_min)/8G_N]。因此在没有"蟒蛇午餐"几何时稳定子复杂度为O(1)，而在存在"蟒蛇午餐"时指数增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解量子态在子区域上的稳定子复杂度与纠缠结构间的关系是量子信息与全息引力交叉领域的重要问题。PET态是研究全息CFT中固定能量边界条件的热态重要模型，而Wigner负性作为可操作的魔幻单调量，能刻画经典模拟量子电路制备该约化态的复杂性。

Method: 作者利用CFT谱的伪随机性假设和重算子插入假设，计算PET态相对微正则密度矩阵的Wigner负性。研究对象是全息对偶中的部分纠缠热态，通过外极值面面积A_out和最小极值面面积A_min来表征Wigner负性的指数形式，进而关联到体几何中"蟒蛇午餐"结构的有无。

Result: Wigner负性为exp[(A_out - A_min)/8G_N]。在没有蟒蛇午餐时，外极值面与最小极值面重合（A_out = A_min），稳定子复杂度为O(1)；而在存在蟒蛇午餐时，稳定子复杂度获得指数增强。

Conclusion: 稳定子复杂度与体几何中的极值面结构密切相关，"蟒蛇午餐"的存在会指数增强边界子区域约化态的稳定子复杂度，暗示了量子引力几何与量子计算复杂性之间的深层联系。

Abstract: In this note, we study the stabilizer complexity of the reduced density matrix corresponding to one side of a partially entangled thermal (PET) state with fixed energy boundary conditions in a holographic CFT. In particular, we study Wigner negativity, an operationally meaningful magic monotone which can be interpreted as the complexity of classically simulating any quantum circuit preparation of the reduced state on the subregion. Using assumptions on the pseudorandomness of the CFT spectrum and the heavy operator insertion, we observe that the Wigner negativity of the PET state relative to the microcanonical density matrix at the given energy is given by $\exp\left[\frac{1}{8G_N}(A_{\text{out}} - A_{\text{min}})\right]$, where $A_{\text{out}}$ is the area of the outer extremal surface, while $A_{\text{min}}$ is the area of the minimal extremal surface. Thus, the stabilizer complexity of the reduced density matrix on the boundary subregion is $O(1)$ in the absence of a python's lunch, but gets exponentially enhanced in the presence of a python's lunch in the bulk geometry.

</details>


### [3] [Constraints on the $O(n)$ model from a negative number of flavors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12481)
*Daniele Artico,Jasper Roosmale Nepveu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文将O(n)模型中味数n视为连续变量，利用O(n)与Sp(-n)的对偶性，在n为负偶数时通过将算子按不可约表示分解，揭示了不同算子标度维度间新的简并性，从而无需额外圈图计算即可从已知结果导出任意φ^k型算子的两圈反常维度，并在特定算子基下得到新的非重整化结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在O(n)模型中，将味数n作为变量处理可以产生非微扰的算子谱约束。但此前这些关系仅对正偶数n成立，缺乏对负偶数n以及一般味结构的系统分析，限制了这些约束在微扰计算中的应用。

Method: 利用O(n)与Sp(-n)的对偶性将味数关系推广到n的负偶数值；将具有一般味结构的算子分解为不可约表示（irreducible representations）；在微扰论中利用该结构揭示不同算子标度维度的简并性；通过简并性机制从现有结果推导任意φ^k型算子的两圈反常维度，无需额外圈图计算；分析重整化群混合矩阵的模式，并讨论框架向OPE系数和时空维度解析延拓（evanescence）的推广。

Result: 发现了对任意n均成立的不同算子标度维度间的简并性；推导出任意φ^k型算子的两圈反常维度；在特定算子基下获得了新的非重整化结果；提出了将框架推广到OPE系数及由时空维度延续产生的消失项（evanescence）约束的途径。

Conclusion: O(n)与Sp(-n)的对偶性结合不可约表示分解是一种强大且系统的工具，能够揭示算子谱中的深层简并结构，显著减少微扰计算的负担，并产生新的非重整化约束，同时框架可进一步扩展到OPE系数和时空维度延续情境。

Abstract: Treating the number of flavors $n$ as a variable in the $O(n)$ model leads to non-perturbative constraints on the spectrum of operators. Using the duality between $O(n)$ and $Sp(-n)$, we extend these relations to negative even values of $n$ and we make them explicit by decomposing operators with general flavor structure into irreducible representations. In perturbation theory, we exploit this structure to reveal novel degeneracies in the scaling dimensions of different operators, which persist for arbitrary $n$. This allows us to derive the two-loop anomalous dimension of any $φ^k$-type operator from existing results without additional loop calculations. The same mechanism dictates patterns in renormalization group mixing matrices, yielding new non-renormalization results in a specific operator basis. We comment on extending this framework to relations between operator product expansion coefficients and to analogous constraints arising from evanescence under continuation in the number of spacetime dimensions.

</details>


### [4] [Type III von Neumann Algebras are Magical](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12512)
*Mudassir Moosa*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文论证了Type III型冯·诺依曼代数从根本上需要无限量的magic（非Clifford门资源）。通过考虑有限维量子系统（如格点系统）的热力学极限，作者证明若热力学极限态仅具有有界的magic，则其局部冯·诺依曼代数不可能为Type III型。这对量子场论的量子模拟有直接影响，因为量子场论局部子区域的代数已知为Type III型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在容错量子计算中，非Clifford门的数量（magic）是关键资源。冯·诺依曼代数是描述无穷维量子系统的形式化框架，其中Type III代数尤为重要（如量子场论中的局部区域代数）。然而Type III代数中密度矩阵和迹等标准概念失效，本文旨在理解Type III代数与magic这一计算资源之间的内在联系。

Method: 作者从有限维量子系统的热力学极限出发，研究当系统趋于无穷大时，若极限态只含有限的magic资源，则对应的局部冯·诺依曼代数具有何种结构。通过对比有界magic态与Type III代数所需的数学性质（如迹和密度矩阵的失效），建立了magic资源与代数类型之间联系的证明框架。

Result: 证明了如果热力学极限中的量子态仅具有有界的magic资源，则其产生的局部冯·诺依曼代数不能是Type III型。由于量子场论局部子区域的代数已知为Type III型，这意味着对量子场论进行量子模拟时，magic资源的需求在热力学极限下本质上是无穷的。

Conclusion: Type III型冯·诺依曼代数本质上要求无穷多的magic资源。这一结果对量子场论的量子模拟具有重要启示：在热力学极限下，模拟量子场论局部区域的性质在magic意义上必然需要无限资源，揭示了magic作为量子计算资源与代数结构深层性质之间的本质联系。

Abstract: The number of non-Clifford gates needed to perform a task, or simply \textit{magic}, is a resource for fault-tolerant quantum computation. Von Neumann algebras provide a formal mathematical structure to describe infinite-dimensional quantum systems, such as those in quantum field theory or quantum statistical mechanics. A particularly important class of von Neumann algebras is called Type III algebras, for which the standard notions of finite-dimensional systems such as density matrices and traces break down. In this work, we argue that Type III von Neumann algebras fundamentally require an infinite amount of magic. Specifically, we consider the thermodynamic limit of finite-dimensional quantum systems, such as lattice systems, and show that if the states in the thermodynamic limit possess only a bounded amount of magic, the resulting local von Neumann algebra cannot be of Type III. Our result has direct implications for the quantum simulations of quantum field theories, for which the algebra of a local subregion is known to be of Type III.

</details>

*Nicola Maggiore*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在Symanzik边界量子场论框架下系统研究了带状区域上的Maxwell-Chern-Simons理论，通过变分原理和边界Ward恒等式导出了容许的边界条件，揭示了带边界U(1)电流代数结构，并利用散射行列式表示得到了有限相互作用能的封闭形式，展示了从长程Casimir力到Yukawa抑制的渡越行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解带边界的有拓扑质量的规范理论（Maxwell-Chern-Simons）的边界物理，包括边界条件、边界电流代数、边缘态色散关系以及相互作用能量的定量行为，特别是在有限宽度带状几何中的拓扑边界数据与动力学体效应之间的相互作用。

Method: 采用Symanzik边界量子场论框架，在一条带上研究Maxwell-Chern-Simons理论；加入二次单切向导数截断下最一般的局域边界泛函；通过局部性与变分原理导出容许边界条件，并通过边界Ward恒等式实现规范对称性；构建边界U(1)电流代数，计算反射系数，推导封闭的行列式（散射）表示以求有限相互作用能。

Result: 条带支撑两条边界U(1)电流代数，其相反能级由Chern-Simons耦合κ和边界取向决定，边缘速度依赖局域边界耦合；翻转对称分支上边缘速度大小相等方向相反。边界数据决定体模反射系数并得到相互作用能的封闭散射行列式表示。在Maxwell极限（κ=0）中边缘间相互作用是长程的并产生幂律Casimir力，而Maxwell-Chern-Simons理论中拓扑质量m=κg²在大间距mh≫1时产生Yukawa抑制。行列式形式提供了渐近区域的解析控制，将拓扑边界数据（能级和异常流）与动力学体效应（色散、边界混合和Casimir相互作用）分离。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了带状Maxwell-Chern-Simons理论边界条件的完整自洽框架，揭示了边界电流代数与Chern-Simons耦合之间的定量联系，并提供了区分拓扑与动力学效应的统一解析工具（散射行列式表示），对理解拓扑边界态的相互作用和Casimir效应具有重要价值。

Abstract: We study Maxwell--Chern--Simons theories on a strip $\mathcal M=\mathbb R^{1,1}\times[0,h]$ within the Symanzik framework for quantum field theory with boundaries. We add the most general local boundary functional within the quadratic one-tangential-derivative truncation adopted throughout the paper and derive the admissible boundary conditions from locality and the variational principle, with gauge symmetry implemented through boundary Ward identities. The strip supports two boundary $U(1)$ current algebras, with opposite levels fixed by the Chern--Simons coupling $κ$ and by boundary orientation, while edge velocities depend on local boundary couplings. They are equal and opposite on the flip-symmetric branch, which is the natural choice for a spatially symmetric strip. The same boundary data determine the reflection coefficients for bulk modes and yield a closed determinant (scattering) representation of the finite interaction energy. In the Maxwell limit ($κ=0$) the inter-edge interaction is long-ranged and produces a power-law Casimir force, whereas in Maxwell--Chern--Simons theory the topological mass $m=κg^2$ generates Yukawa suppression at large separations $mh\gg1$. The determinant form also gives analytic control of the asymptotic regimes and separates topological boundary data (levels and anomaly inflow) from dynamical bulk effects (dispersion, boundary mixing and Casimir interaction).

</details>


### [6] [D4-branes wrapped on topological disks from matter-coupled F(4) gauged supergravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12850)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索超对称AdS/CFT对应中的新型几何解，特别是涉及半纺锤与拓扑圆盘这类非平凡基底上的超引力解，以理解五维N=2 SCFT在三维紧致化后产生的共形缺陷结构。

Method: 在SO(3)×SO(3)规范群的物质耦合F(4)规范超引力框架下，研究U(1)×U(1)子扇区中的AdS₄×Σ解，将其提升到十维巨IIA型理论中并解释为D4-D8膜缠绕系统，同时分析解的渐近行为与全息自由能。

Result: 找到了一系列保持八个超电荷的解，具有SO(2)×SO(2)或SO(2)_diag对称性。部分解渐近于局部AdS₆几何，对应五维N=2 SCFT中的余维-2共形缺陷，其全息自由能发散；另一些解具有有限自由能。这些结果既推广了已知解，又提供了新颖类型的解。

Conclusion: 本文的新解扩展了此前关于F(4)规范超引力中AdS₄×Σ解的研究，阐明了这些解与五维N=2 SCFT共形缺陷之间的对偶关系，并区分了发散与有限自由能两类解的物理特性。

Abstract: We study a number of supersymmetric $AdS_4\times Σ$ solutions with $Σ$ being a topological disk with non-trivial $U(1)$ holonomy on the boundary or a ``half-spindle'' from matter-coupled $F(4)$ gauged supergravity. The gauged supergravity is coupled to three vector multiplets with $SO(3)\times SO(3)$ gauge group. The resulting solutions preserve eight supercharges and $SO(2)\times SO(2)$ or $SO(2)_{\text{diag}}$ symmetries and are expected to be dual to $N=2$ SCFTs in three dimensions arising from compactifications of five-dimensional $N=2$ SCFT on a half-spindle. All of the solutions lie within the $U(1)\times U(1)$ subsector of the matter-coupled $F(4)$ gauged supergravity that can be embedded in massive type IIA theory. After uplifted to ten dimensions, the solutions can be interpreted as a system of D4-D8-branes wrapped on $Σ$. Some of the solutions are asymptotic to a locally $AdS_6$ geometry and can be identified as codimension-2 conformal defects within the $N=2$ SCFT in five dimensions. For these solutions, the circle inside $Σ$ decompactifies in the $AdS_6$ limit rendering the holographic free energy infinite. There also exist solutions with finite free energy in the dual three-dimensional SCFTs. In addition, we show that the results both extend the previously known solutions and provide a novel class of solutions.

</details>


### [7] [Limits of the inverse scattering problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13105)
*Matvei Fedin,Kirill Gubarev,Andrey Morozov*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文探讨了将传统X射线/拉东变换层析成像推广到粒子情形的问题，核心是研究粒子速度可以低到何种程度，以保证从线积分测量中重建势函数的可行性。通过理论示例和机器学习管线两种方法对这一问题进行了研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统层析成像基于X射线/拉东逆变换重建密度函数，但当研究对象从光子换为粒子时，只有在粒子速度足够高（近似直线运动）时问题才能退化为经典情形。因此需要回答：给定势函数参数，粒子速度低到多少时仍能完成重建？

Method: 采用两种互补方法：一是构建简单的理论示例进行解析分析，二是设计基于机器学习的管线来恢复势函数，通过实验验证不同粒子速度下的重建可行性边界。

Result: 文中给出了粒子速度下限与势函数参数之间的依赖关系，并通过ML管线证实了在低于一定速度时重建变得不可行或退化。

Conclusion: 粒子速度与势函数参数共同决定了粒子层析成像的可重建性边界，这为实际应用中的粒子层析成像系统设计提供了理论指导。

Abstract: The main goal of tomography is the reconstruction of density function out of its line integrals (integral measurements of this density along x-ray lines). Such construction is possible and known as inverse x-ray/Radon transformations. We are interested in generalization of this problem to the case of particles. For this we need to limit the speed of these particles, otherwise the problem is reduced to the previous one. The question we discuss in this paper is how low can this speed be depending on the parameters of the studied potential. We study this problem using simple theoretical examples and Machine Learning pipeline to restore the potential.

</details>


### [8] [Exact Evaluation of Lattice-Regularized Scalar Field Vacuum Amplitude via Site Permutations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13117)
*Vadim Asnin*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 提出了一种在任意维数格点、任意势能条件下计算单标量场真空振幅的新方法，通过对路径积分中的动能指数因子进行对称化处理，并利用Waring分解来简化计算。


<details>

Abstract: We investigate the collision dynamics of two bright sphalerons in a (1+1)-dimensional deformed $φ^6$ scalar field theory with a symmetric potential possessing false vacua. Two one-parameter realizations of the model, referred to as the barrier and well models, are considered and their static and linear instability properties are first reviewed. We then study head-on collisions of boosted sphalerons over a broad range of initial velocities and deformation parameters. The scattering dynamics exhibit a rich variety of final states, including the production of kink-antikink pairs, long-lived oscillons in true and false vacuum, multiple oscillons propagating in false-vacuum regions, and radiative decay. A particularly remarkable outcome is the emergence of a long-lived bubble of the false broken vacuum bounded by a kink-antikink pair, which repeatedly collapses and re-expands before eventually decaying into an oscillon. Abstract: The distance matrix of a finite point cloud can be visualized as a heatmap. When the data arise from a time series, the sublevel sets of this image are known as recurrence plots and are widely used in time series analysis. Abstract: Uncertainty in nonlinear dynamical systems is often organized by transport structures that are not apparent from posterior geometry alone. We introduce Dynamical Uncertainty Geometry (DUG), a framework that separates three ingredients that are frequently conflated: posterior uncertainty, future observations, and model-induced transport. DUG indexes posterior credible sets and transport-conditioned subsets by a common enclosed-probability coordinate, allowing physically meaningful classes to be tracked across credibility levels rather than examined at a single threshold. The framework combines a mass-ranked credible filtration, a description of future experiments through their induced probability laws and Fisher geometry, and a labelled transport skeleton representing dynamically distinct outcomes. We establish stability results for the resulting mass-indexed persistence modules under simultaneous perturbations of posterior density and probability measure, including continuum, refinement, and finite atomic formulations. These results provide quantitative control of persistence computed from numerical approximations and weighted grids. Two benchmark problems illustrate the framework. In a short-arc orbit-determination setting, DUG identifies dynamically distinct return classes within a connected uncertainty region and highlights a difference between information-based and local Fisher-based observation-design criteria. In the Earth-Moon planar circular restricted three-body problem, DUG reveals multiple first-hit transport outcomes coexisting within a connected credible region and provides diagnostics for assessing their numerical resolution. Together, these examples show how topological summaries of posterior geometry, when coupled to transport labels and future experiments, yield a richer description of uncertainty than either posterior probabilities or dynamical classifications alone.

Finally, we identify the necessary performance metrics that will enable error-correction thresholds at high qubit densities, illuminating a path towards scalable quantum information processing.

Abstract: Understanding and coherently controlling the properties of interacting quantum many-body systems is a central challenge in non-equilibrium quantum physics. In the XXX limit, these states exhibit exact plateaus in both entanglement and stabilizer Rényi entropy and correspond to coherent superpositions of long-range valence-bond solid (VBS) states. We further show that pulse engineering preserves the distinct chaotic and non-chaotic regimes of the original model, while that is largely absent in the dynamical generation of entanglement and nonstabilizerness. The pulse-engineered models generate nearly identical quantum resources in both regimes, revealing a partial decoupling between quantum chaos and quantum-resource generation. Across the cycles examined, flux can either enhance the maximal Leggett--Garg violation or shift strong violations to earlier measurement times, with half flux driving the four-site cycle to the Lüders bound. These results establish gauge-invariant flux as a resource for engineering local measurement back-action and temporal quantum correlations.

Abstract: Free-space quantum communication provides a flexible complement to fiber-based quantum networks, but its point-to-point capacity is fundamentally limited by diffraction, atmospheric extinction and beam wandering induced by turbulence. In this work, we study the end-to-end performance of large-scale free-space quantum networks connecting randomly distributed fixed or mobile users, modelled as Waxman random graphs. We derive the mean network capacity, edge consumption and connectivity phase transitions for both single-path and multi-path (flooding) routing. We prove that any optimal backbone configuration must correspond to a capacity-maximizing Voronoi tessellation of the network region, and show that this can be efficiently approximated by a centroidal Voronoi tessellation via Lloyd's algorithm, with backbone nodes connected according to a Delaunay triangulation. Numerical results show that even a modest number of backbone nodes substantially improves end-to-end capacity and reduces edge consumption for both mobile and fixed users.

Abstract: Quantum subspace diagonalization methods are promising algorithms for quantum chemistry on near-term quantum computers. Abstract: Whether neutral-atom quantum optimization protocols exhibit genuine concentration toward low-energy solution structure remains an open question. By contrast, for relaxed targets, the shot cost becomes effectively constant, and the corresponding quantum enhancement diminishes, with the classical postprocessing heuristic alone reaching the target in order-unity attempts. Together, these results establish an operational method for quantifying quantum optimization performance and clarify the regimes under which quantum approaches can yield practical benefits.

By introducing a zero-noise extrapolation method without additional circuit-folding overhead, we accurately execute deep error-circuits with entangling-gate counts beyond those typical of Hadamard test circuits. We accurately reconstruct the time evolution across multiple timesteps despite hardware noise and finite device coherence. Abstract: Determining whether a target unitary can be implemented within a prescribed quantum circuit architecture is a fundamental problem in quantum information, with direct implications for optimisation and compilation of quantum circuits, and hardware-efficient quantum computation. We formulate the witness construction as a semidefinite program by maximising the fidelity between the Choi state of the target unitary and those of tested circuits. The resulting witnesses provide practical and quantitative certificates of incompatibility, implying lower bounds on implementation resources such as the gate count or circuit depth, and can also be used experimentally to benchmark quantum devices by certifying that an implemented unitary channel goes beyond the capabilities of a given circuit architecture. For Clifford unitaries, we exploit the stabiliser formalism to reduce the construction to linear programming, enabling both more efficient numerical certification for circuits containing on the order of seven two-qubit gates, and analytical witnesses for some families of architectures made of an arbitrary number of gates.

Abstract: Discrete-time quantum walks provide a versatile framework for investigating the generation, redistribution, and transport of quantum correlations in composite quantum systems. By employing logarithmic negativity and the generalized geometric measure (GGM), we systematically characterize the redistribution of bipartite entanglement among different subsystem partitions and the emergence of genuine multipartite entanglement involving the two coin and two position degrees of freedom. We show that the entanglement dynamics are strongly influenced by the lattice topology. The open-boundary regime exhibits a monotonic redistribution of quantum correlations, whereas the closed-boundary regime gives rise to pronounced oscillatory behavior due to boundary-induced interference and recurrent wave-packet overlap. In the open-boundary regime, the GGM rapidly approaches its theoretical maximum value of $1/2$ and remains largely insensitive to the choice of the initial Bell state as well as to continuous variations of the local coin operator over a broad parameter range, except near the Pauli-$X$ coin. These results demonstrate that maximal genuine multipartite entanglement generation is a robust and generic feature of open-boundary two-walker discrete-time quantum walks, establishing them as promising platforms for engineering multipartite quantum correlations in quantum information processing and quantum simulation.

We validate our framework on three independent public multi-modal imaging datasets covering fluorescence (BBBC021), bright-field (BBBC041) and histopathology (PathMNIST). QPBC consistently resolves angular-phase order and discriminates distinct biological phenotypes with high statistical significance. After principal-axis alignment, the optimal probing frequency universally converges, driven by Fourier directional sensitivity; negative-control experiments fully eliminate discriminative capacity, demonstrating frequency tuning acts as an on-off switch. Cross-dataset benchmarks confirm QPBC outperforms conventional Fourier-phase statistics, where inherent inversion symmetry serves as a built-in pipeline self-check. QPBC delivers a universal, classically unachievable quantitative texture observable, establishes interpretable quantum morphometry, and broadens the toolbox for quantum-inspired analysis applicable to diverse multi-modal microscopic measurements.

Abstract: Magic plays a dual role in quantum computation: it promotes stabilizer dynamics from efficient classical simulability to universality, but it presents a central challenge for fault tolerance, since non-stabilizer operations are harder to protect against noise. Magic state distillation addresses this issue; however, existing protocols typically assume prior structure in the input, such as proximity to the target or a specified noise model. Here we introduce universal magic state concentration: a fixed stabilizer protocol that converts a few copies of an unknown pure non-stabilizer qubit state into an exact target magic state. Motivated by the obstruction to exact $T$-state concentration, we show that $\mathrm{CCZ}$ states behave fundamentally differently. Six input copies are necessary and sufficient to distill one exact $\mathrm{CCZ}$ state, with an optimal success probability determined by the linearized order-three stabilizer Rényi entropy $M^{\mathrm{lin}}_3$. Beyond this, we show that $M^{\mathrm{lin}}_3$ governs the optimal state dependence of any protocol up to nine input copies, and we showcase an eight-copy protocol with improved success probability. Furthermore, block repetition of our protocols yields asymptotic distillation rates that achieve optimal scaling up to logarithmic factors. As a corollary, any unknown pure qubit magic state suffices for universal quantum computation via exact $\mathrm{CCZ}$ injection. Together, these results identify the stabilizer Rényi entropy as a fundamental operational quantity in magic state distillation.

Conclusion: 结果表明，近期可得的量子技术能够指数级增强我们学习经典信号的能力，QΨ理论为在实用实验任务中系统识别严格量子优势提供了统一框架，打破了量子Fisher信息的局限。

Abstract: Quantum technology has the potential to transform scientific discovery, but quantum advantages often require processing capabilities well beyond the reach of experimental platforms. We show that coupling a single controllable qubit to an otherwise conventional sensor can exponentially reduce the number of measurements required to learn classical signals. These rigorous quantum advantages apply to fundamental sensing tasks, including learning Fourier coefficients, extracting temporal correlations from time-varying signals, and estimating transformations of physical observables. Using a superconducting cavity--qubit architecture, we experimentally demonstrate $10^7$-fold reductions in the number of measurements required for Fourier-amplitude and time-varying signal learning. Our $\textit{quantum feature sensing}$ algorithms further enable orders-of-magnitude improvements in simulations of weak-signal dark matter detection and wireless communication applications. These quantum advantages are derived from Quantum Phase-Space Inference (Q$Ψ$), a unifying theory of quantum-enhanced experiments that simultaneously converts a set of experimental objectives and constraints into tight lower bounds and optimal quantum-enhanced learning algorithms while producing a certificate of quantum advantage. Q$Ψ$ extends beyond the regimes captured by quantum Fisher information and provides a framework for systematically identifying rigorous quantum advantages in practical experimental tasks. Together, our results establish that near-term quantum technology can exponentially enhance our ability to learn from classical signals.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 研究表明，即使允许使用来自子群之外的"环境"幺正（包括作用于辅助量子比特的情况），亚线性深度的局域最近邻幺正电路系综仍无法实现上述群上的近似设计。这揭示了这些子群情形与完全酉群之间的指数级分离是根本性的；同时，这带来了量子层析和基准测试方案中显著的电路深度开销，并证明已知的线性深度设计构造在常数因子意义下是最优的。

Abstract: Characterising the efficiency with which designs over various subsets of the unitary group may be constructed is an important goal of quantum information theory. While it is now known that approximate unitary designs can be realised in depth logarithmic in the system size, it has recently been shown that ensembles of local nearest-neighbour sublinear-depth one-dimensional circuits over the matchgate, orthogonal, and symplectic groups cannot form approximate 2-designs over their parent groups; similarly, sublinear-depth ensembles of Cliffords cannot form a Clifford 4-design. In this note we show that this remarkable exponential separation is not merely an artefact of restricting to ensembles consisting of unitaries from the subgroups themselves, but rather that no ensemble of local nearest-neighbour sublinear-depth unitaries can realise approximate designs in the aforementioned cases, even when employing "ambient" unitaries from beyond the subgroup itself (possibly acting on ancilla qubits). This implies that various natural tomography and benchmarking schemes which involves sampling from these groups suffer from a dramatic circuit depth overhead compared to similar protocols which involve sampling from the full unitary group. We additionally conclude that, in all of the above cases, the known linear-depth design constructions are up to constant factors optimal.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的量子动力学模拟算法（从哈密顿模拟到线性和非线性微分方程求解器）都局限于马尔可夫动力学，即系统的未来演化仅依赖于当前状态。然而实际中许多系统（如开放量子系统、流体动力学）具有非马尔可夫特性，其未来演化依赖于过去的历史状态（具有记忆效应）。作者旨在填补这一空白，开发针对非马尔可夫动力系统的高效量子算法。

Method: 本文提出两类方法：(1) 对于一般卷积核的VIDEs，在M<1条件下（记忆项强度弱于马尔可夫部分耗散）开发高效量子算法，并给出了M≥1时的下界；(2) 对于结构化核（可简洁地分解为指数函数的叠加），提出"马尔可夫化"（Markovianization）过程，将VIDEs转化为一组更大的ODE集合，从而即使在M≥1时也能利用现有的量子ODE求解技术进行高效模拟。

Result: 给定的问题输入具有高效电路的前提下，算法在系统规模上相对于经典算法实现了指数级加速。对于一般核且M≥1的情况，证明了该问题对一族系统是不可解的。通过对结构化核的马尔可夫化，突破了M≥1的限制，使得更广泛的非马尔可夫系统可被量子计算机高效模拟。

Conclusion: 本文成果将量子计算机可高效模拟的动力学系统范围从马尔可夫系统拓展到了非马尔可夫系统（包括具有记忆的Volterra积分-微分方程），并在Mori-Zwanzig形式体系（用于开放量子系统和流体动力学）中得到了应用，为量子模拟的适用领域打开了新局面。

Abstract: Existing quantum algorithms for simulating dynamical systems -- from Hamiltonian simulation to linear and nonlinear differential equations solvers -- simulate Markovian dynamics, in which the system's future evolution depends solely on its current state. We turn our attention to developing quantum algorithms for non-Markovian dynamical systems where the system's future evolution depends on its past history and thus has memory. Specifically, we develop efficient algorithms for linear Volterra integro-differential equations (VIDEs) with a convolution memory kernel that output a quantum state encoding the state description over a time interval or at a particular time. Given efficient circuits for the problem inputs, our algorithms achieve an exponential speedup in system size over existing classical algorithms.
  We develop an algorithm for general kernels assuming that $\textsf{M} < 1$, where $\textsf{M}$ characterizes the strength of the memory term relative to the dissipation of the Markovian part of the dynamics. We complement this with lower bounds for general-kernel VIDEs when $\textsf{M} \geq 1$, showing that the problem becomes intractable for a family of systems. However, by specializing to structured kernels which admit concise decompositions over exponentials, we develop efficient quantum algorithms even when $\textsf M \geq 1$ by converting the VIDE into a larger set of ODEs, a procedure which we call Markovianization. As an application of the overall framework, we discuss the Mori-Zwanzig formalism used in open quantum systems and fluid dynamics. Overall, our results expand the range of dynamical systems that quantum computers can simulate efficiently.

</details>

Method: 本文考虑局部和非局部连接均任意受限的情况，基于分块矩阵高斯消元法给出分布式CNOT和Clifford电路的综合方法。通过推广泡利指数电路表示，将方法扩展到所有Clifford+RZ电路，并自然地与现有的T-count优化方法集成。

Result: 该综合方法在渐近意义下最优。作为应用，展示了如何在CSS码中（将n个逻辑量子比特编码在k个块中）使用O(nk)个跨块横向CNOT和块内泡利测量来实现CNOT电路。

Conclusion: 该工作为受限连接条件下的分布式量子电路综合提供了渐近最优的解决方案，并可与T-count优化方法自然结合，为大规模容错量子计算中的非局部操作最小化提供了理论保证。

Abstract: To achieve large-scale fault-tolerant quantum computation, it may be easier to combine many small sets of qubits than to construct a single large set. For example via quantum error correction with block codes, or distributed quantum processors utilizing shared entanglement. In these regimes, the time or error budget of the overall quantum computation may be dominated by non-local operations. Hence, it is worthwhile to minimize the number of these operations. We consider the case where both non-local and local connectivity may be arbitrarily restricted, and give an asymptotically optimal synthesis method for distributed CNOT and Clifford circuits, based on block-matrix Gaussian elimination. We extend this to all Clifford+RZ circuits by generalizing the Pauli exponential circuit representation; this naturally integrates with existing methods for optimizing T-count. As an application, we show how to implement CNOT circuits in a CSS code encoding n logical qubits in k blocks using O(nk) inter-block transversal CNOTs and intra-block Pauli measurements.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文报道了一种基于量子SWITCH的BB84类量子密码实验实现，通过将Alice和Bob的测量-制备操作置于量子SWITCH中，实现不定因果序，使平均窃听者检测概率达到0.15±0.02。与传统BB84不同，该方法通过测量控制量子位检测窃听，无需公开和丢弃密钥比特，每个保留量子比特原则上都可被检测窃听同时用于密钥生成。尽管当前实现因后选择需求尚不构成安全QKD协议，但它提供了利用不定因果序在不牺牲密钥比特情况下检测窃听的概念验证。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索量子物理学中操作顺序叠加（不定因果序）这一新型量子资源的实际应用价值，特别是利用量子SWITCH实现一种无需牺牲密钥比特即可检测窃听的新型量子密码协议，克服传统BB84协议中必须公开披露并丢弃部分密钥以检测窃听的局限性。

Method: 实验采用光量子SWITCH结构，将Alice和Bob的测量-制备操作嵌入其中形成不定因果序。通过测量控制量子位来检测窃听者，而非比较密钥。实验还开发了一种新测量技术，允许在量子SWITCH内部测量光子偏振而不破坏路径相干性。

Result: 实现了平均窃听者检测概率为0.15±0.02（每共享量子比特），窃听检测通过控制量子位测量完成，无需公开密钥材料。每个保留量子比特原则上可同时用于窃听检测和密钥生成。

Conclusion: 该实验证明了利用不定因果序可以在不牺牲密钥比特的情况下检测窃听，但当前实现因后选择需求尚不构成安全的QKD协议。尽管如此，它为不定因果序作为量子资源在密码学中的应用提供了概念验证。

Abstract: In quantum physics the order in which different operations occur can be placed in superposition. The resulting processes have an indefinite causal order and are both of fundamental interest and can be viewed as a novel quantum resource that enables a variety of new protocols. Here we report an experimental implementation of one such protocol, where we perform BB84-like quantum cryptography by placing Alice and Bob's measurement-and-preparation operations in a photonic quantum SWITCH. By embedding Alice and Bob within the quantum SWITCH, the protocol achieves an average eavesdropper detection probability of $0.15 \pm 0.02$ per shared qubit, with eavesdropper detection performed through measurements of the control qubit rather than by comparing the key. Unlike the standard BB84 and related schemes, which detect eavesdropping by publicly revealing and discarding a fraction of the raw key, our approach requires no disclosure of key material: every retained qubit can, in principle, be tested for eavesdropping while remaining available for key generation. The experiment relies on a new measurement technique that allows the polarization of a photon to be measured inside the quantum SWITCH without destroying path coherence. Although the present implementation does not yet constitute a secure quantum key distribution protocol, owing to the post-selection required for measurements within the quantum SWITCH, it provides a proof of principle that indefinite causal order can be exploited to detect eavesdropping without sacrificing key bits.

</details>

Conclusion: 该工作首次在可展不可约情形下完整建立了quiver突变类到拓扑类型的不变映射，为奇点分类理论提供了新的代数不变量，并展示了dg导出范畴中等变配对技术的强大应用潜力。

Abstract: The main result of this article shows that the quiver mutation class of a malleable real Morsification uniquely determines the integral monodromy module of a plane curve singularity. In particular, we show that the quiver mutation class of a malleable divide determines the complex topological type of an irreducible plane curve singularity. This establishes the algebraic-to-topological implication of a conjecture of S.~Fomin, P.~Pylyavskyy, E.~Shustin and D.~Thurston in the malleable irreducible case. The result is proven by developing representation-theoretic techniques based on an equivariant Euler pairing in the derived category of continuous finite-dimensional dg modules over a differential bigraded Ginzburg algebra. A key step uses these techniques to show that the quiver mutation class of a plabic fence uniquely recovers the torsion part of the Alexander module of the associated smooth link.

Result: 证明了Calabi-Yau流形不同胚于弱Fano流形，且该阻碍适用于具拟正全纯截面曲率的Kähler流形；排除了具有Hamiltonian圆作用（固定点孤立）的辛流形；并证明了Calabi-Yau流形的基本群不与Kähler双曲流形的基本群同构。

Abstract: We study whether the topology underlying a Calabi-Yau manifold can support natural geometric positivity or negativity structures. In even complex dimension $n \geq 4$ (assuming $b_2=1$ when $n \geq 6$), we strengthen a theorem of Oguiso-Peternell by proving that a Calabi-Yau manifold is not homeomorphic to a weak Fano $n$-fold. The same obstruction applies to Kähler manifolds with quasi-positive holomorphic sectional curvature. A transformation-group analogue excludes, in particular, symplectic manifolds admitting Hamiltonian circle actions with isolated fixed points. On the negative side, we show that the fundamental group of a Calabi-Yau manifold is not isomorphic to that of a Kähler hyperbolic manifold. Taken together, these results exhibit a common topological rigidity separating Calabi-Yau manifolds from several fundamental classes governed by geometric positivity or negativity.

For each finite collision complex, we factor the reduction morphism into elementary wall crossings and obtain a reconstruction formula in terms of ordinary Witten-Kontsevich correlators. *Thomas Prellberg*

Conclusion: 本文建立了碰撞生成压缩原理，为Keller映射的压缩现象提供了统一的代数解释，同时构造反例表明Zhao湮灭猜想并非普遍成立。作者明确指出未声称全局极小性，说明结论在特定映射类中的局部有效性。该工作深化了对Jacobian猜想退化结构的理解，并为后续研究提供了可重复验证的精确计算框架。

Abstract: We formulate an elementary collision-generated compression principle for homogeneous Keller maps. In the language of polarization algebras, the construction takes the subalgebra generated by a collision; the additional point is that this subalgebra carries a noninjective Keller restriction and controls the dimension of the standard symmetric lift. For Thompson's 24-variable cubic-homogeneous map, exact polarization gives growth \(2,4,11,20,20\), and the resulting subalgebra is exactly MacFarlane's 20-dimensional invariant subspace. Thus the known \(24\)-to-\(20\) compression is recovered canonically from the collision itself. Although the resulting 40-variable lift had already been noted, we give an explicit 350-monomial homogeneous quartic for which Zhao's Vanishing Conjecture fails, together with an exact gradient collision over \(\Q(i)\). Over \(\Q\), \(\Q(i)\), and \(\C\), no proper invariant linear subspace containing this collision is available before the unchanged symmetric lift. No global minimality is claimed. The principal explicit calculations are checked by an accompanying exact calculation.

</details>


### [91] [Rigidity for Lie algebras of locally finite derivations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12567)

TL;DR: 本文证明：域上有限生成交换代数的Derivation代数的有限生成Lie子代数，若其元素在代数上局部有限，则该Lie子代数必为有限维。由此刻画了有限多个局部有限Derivation生成的Lie子代数的有限维性等价条件，并推广到仿射簇自同构群及多项式控制系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究由局部有限Derivation生成的Lie子代数的结构性质，回答Popov（2005年）提出的关于两个单幂单参数子群生成群的可代数性问题，并将代数结构理论应用于多项式控制系统的有限维双线性实现。

Result: （1）有限生成交换代数上所有元素局部有限的有限生成Lie子代数必为有限维；（2）局部幂零Derivation生成的Lie子代数可解，在约化情形下导长≤dim A且最优；（3）对仿射簇X，有限多个连通代数子群生成的Aut(X)子群可代数当且仅当其切代数生成的Lie代数的所有元素局部有限；（4）对两个单幂单参数子群的情形，回答了Popov 2005年的问题；（5）给出了多项式控制系统具有含状态坐标的多项式观测量的精确有限维双线性实现的刻画。

Conclusion: 局部有限Derivation的有限生成Lie子代数的有限维性得到了完整刻画，核心工具（局部幂零Derivation的Lie子代数可解性）是独立且有独立价值的定理。该结果统一了代数群自同构的理论研究并提供了控制论应用。

Abstract: Let $A$ be a finitely generated commutative algebra over a field $\mathbb K$ of characteristic zero. We prove that every finitely generated Lie subalgebra $L\subseteq\operatorname{Der}_{\mathbb K}(A)$ whose elements are locally finite on $A$ is finite-dimensional. Consequently, for a Lie subalgebra generated by finitely many locally finite derivations, the following are equivalent: it is finite-dimensional, it acts locally finitely on $A$, and all its elements are locally finite. A key ingredient is a second theorem of independent interest: every Lie subalgebra of $\operatorname{Der}_{\mathbb K}(A)$ whose elements are locally nilpotent is solvable; when $A$ is reduced, its derived length is at most $\dim A$, and this bound is sharp. For an affine variety $X$ over an algebraically closed field, we deduce that a subgroup of $\operatorname{Aut}(X)$ generated by finitely many connected algebraic subgroups is algebraic if and only if every element of the Lie algebra generated by their tangent algebras is locally finite. For two unipotent one-parameter subgroups, this provides an answer to a problem posed by Popov in 2005. Our results also characterize polynomial control systems admitting an exact finite-dimensional bilinear realization by polynomial observables containing the state coordinates. The proofs rest on the introduction of a cofinite ideal meeting the closure of every associated point of $\operatorname{Spec} A$: on the subalgebra of derivations vanishing to second order along the corresponding finite subscheme, local finiteness forces local nilpotence. The intersection of that subalgebra with $L$ is therefore solvable by the second theorem, and has finite codimension in $L$; together with local finiteness of the adjoint action, this yields finite-dimensionality.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究动机在于对非阿基米德刚体空间的基本群有限性进行系统研究。此前关于刚体空间基本群拓扑有限生成的结论仅在代数闭域等特殊情形下已知，作者希望将这一有限性结果推广到更一般的非阿基米德域（如局部域），并进一步探索拓扑有限表现性，这需要引入tame étale基本群这一新工具。

Method: 论文采用三种关键技术：（1）将对数几何技术扩展到超越通常有限生成幺半群的更广范围，从而处理严格半稳定形式概形的tame log étale基本群；（2）建立tame log étale基本群的有限性命题并将其与tame étale基本群建立类比联系；（3）利用adic空间映射的"垂直紧化"技术来处理几何构造中的紧化问题。

Result: 主要结果包括：（1）若X是qcqs且K具有拓扑有限生成的tame Galois群（例如代数闭域或局部域），则π₁ᵗ(X/K)拓扑有限生成；（2）若X进一步是严格半稳定形式概形的一般纤维，且其特殊纤维的光滑闭包容许射影snc紧化，则π₁ᵗ(X/K)拓扑有限表现。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了刚体空间tame étale基本群在较一般非阿基米德域上的拓扑有限生成与有限表现定理，拓展了对数几何的适用范围，为理解刚体空间的基本群算术性质提供了新框架，并可与代数几何中的相应有限性结果相对应。

Abstract: We introduce the tame étale fundamental group $π_1^t(X/K)$ of a rigid space X over a non-archimedean field K. We show that if X is qcqs and K has topologically finitely generated tame Galois group (e.g. algebraically closed or a local field), then $π_1^t(X/K)$ is topologically finitely generated. If X is moreover the rigid generic fibre of a strictly semistable formal scheme such that the smooth locus of its special fibre admits a projective snc compactification, then $π_1^t(X/K)$ is topologically finitely presented. The proofs rely on techniques of logarithmic geometry (extended beyond its usual scope of finitely generated monoids), in particular on an analogous finiteness statement for the tame log étale fundamental group, and on the 'vertical compactification' of a map of adic spaces.

</details>


### [93] [Supersymmetry, differential operators of infinite order and theta functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12846)

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文用超对称性重新解读了Sato等人提出的利用无穷阶微分算子（DOI）证明Theta零值模形式性的方法。作者发现，任何奇超对称生成元的"朴素指数"天然就是DOI，Riemann theta函数由超群OSp(1|2n)及其metaplectic覆盖在Siegel平面的超加厚上控制，其中n=2对应3维N=1超共形群和自由无质量标量超多重态，n>2则得到拉格朗日Grassmannian上广义共形结构的超推广。此外，"在壳"作用的奇超对称生成元满足偶型Heisenberg交换关系，运动方程升级为对应osp(1|2n)超Weil表示的BGG型分辨的微分算子复形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Sato于1972年提出了一种通过模变量中的无穷阶微分算子（DOI）来表征Theta零值并证明其模形式性的方法，但该方法在理论框架上缺乏自然统一的解释。本文旨在用超对称这一现代数学物理框架来重新诠释这一方法，为DOI提供自然来源，并揭示其与超群结构、超共形几何及表示论的深层联系。

Method: 本文将Sato的DOI方法置于超对称框架中：将任意奇超对称生成元的朴素指数识别为DOI（因其在导数上的衰减足够快，构成层的态射）。对亏格n的Riemann theta函数，构造自然作用于Siegel平面超加厚上的超群OSp(1|2n)及其metaplectic覆盖。对于n=2，识别出3维N=1超共形群及自由无质量标量超多重态（统一Laplace与Dirac方程）；对n>2，在拉格朗日Grassmannian（作为Hermitian对称空间）上已有的广义共形结构上构造超推广。进一步分析"在壳"情形下奇生成元的Heisenberg交换关系，并将其运动方程升级为对应osp(1|2n)超Weil表示的BGG型分辨复形。

Result: 论文取得了三项核心结果：（1）建立了DOI与超对称生成元之间的自然对应，为Sato方法提供了超对称诠释；（2）系统地描述了OSp(1|2n)超群在Riemann theta函数情形中的控制作用，n=2时统一了Laplace与Dirac方程于超多重态框架中，n>2时给出了广义共形结构的超推广；（3）揭示了"在壳"奇超对称生成元满足偶型Heisenberg关系的非平凡现象，并将其对应的运动方程构造为BGG型分辨的微分算子复形。

Conclusion: 该工作表明超对称性为Sato的DOI方法提供了自然且统一的数学框架，将theta函数的模形式性、超群表示论与微分算子复形理论有机地联系起来。这一视角不仅深化了对Sato方法本质的理解，也为研究Hermitian对称空间上的广义共形结构及其超推广开辟了新途径，并暗示了超Weil表示与BGG分辨之间的深刻联系。

Abstract: In 1972, M. Sato proposed an approach to proving modularity of forms like Thetanullwerte by characterizing them via certain differential operators of infinite order (DOI) in the modular variable(s) alone. A DOI is an infinite series in derivatives decreasing so fast that it acts on holomorphic functions by a sheaf morphism. This approach was developed by several authors including Kashiwara, Kawai, Takei and Yoshida.
  We give an interpretation of this approach using supersymmetry which provides a natural source of DOIs: the naive exponential of any odd supersymmetry generator is a DOI. The case of the Riemann theta function of genus n is governed by the supergroup OSp(1|2n) (and its metaplectic cover) acting on a natural super-thickening of the Siegel plane. For n=2 this is the 3-dimensional N=1 superconformal group and the structure at hand is precisely the free massless scalar supermultiplet (combining the Laplace and Dirac equations). For n>2 we get a super-extension of the generalized conformal structure existing on the Lagrangian Grassmannian as on any Hermitian symmetric space.
  An additional interesting feature here is that the odd supersymmetry generators acting ``on-shell'' (i.e., in the space of solutions of the equations of motion) satisfy even-style Heisenberg commutation relations. These equations of motion upgrade to a complex of differential operators corresponding to a natural BGG-type resolution of the super-Weil representation of osp(1|2n).

</details>

Method: 本文采用代数几何与周群理论的方法。具体而言：(1) 在Y×Y上显式构造一个维数2的代数闭链，通过计算其在Abel-Jacobi映射下的像为零（即Abel-Jacobi平凡），但该闭链在Chow群A^4(Y×Y)中非扭（非零且无限阶），从而利用Chow群与上同调/中间雅可比之间的精确序列关系导出矛盾；(2) 借助Shen-Vial关于乘法Chow-Künneth分解的判据，展示该闭链的存在排除了Y具有乘法Chow-Künneth分解的可能性；(3) 对一般Fano三维簇，采用模代数等价的Chow群版本（即Fourier表示与Griffiths群消没等方法），证明乘法Chow-Künneth分解在代数等价意义下总是存在。

Result: 主要结果：(1) 对亏格7的非常一般素数Fano三维簇Y，构造了A^4(Y×Y)中一个Abel-Jacobi平凡的非扭转闭链，由此证明Y不具乘法Chow-Künneth分解；(2) 对任意Fano三维簇，证明了其在模代数等价的Chow群下具有乘法Chow-Künneth分解。

Conclusion: 本文通过显式构造和非平凡闭链计算，严格确立了亏格7素数Fano三维簇在精确（非模等价）意义下缺乏乘法Chow-Künneth分解，填补了该领域的一个关键空白；同时证明了模代数等价的正面结果，表明这一障碍本质源于Chow群的超越（而非代数）部分，深化了对Fano三维簇Chow环乘法结构的理解。

Abstract: Let $Y$ be a very general prime Fano threefold of genus 7. We exhibit an explicit 2-cycle on $Y\times Y$ that is Abel-Jacobi trivial but non-torsion in the Chow group $A^4(Y\times Y)$. As a consequence, $Y$ does not admit a multiplicative Chow-Künneth decomposition, in the sense of Shen-Vial. We also show that any Fano threefold has a multiplicative Chow-Künneth decomposition modulo algebraic equivalence.

</details>

Method: 采用热带枚举方法，对平面良间距（well-spaced）椭圆曲线进行计数，并利用Cela-Koyama最近建立的亏格1对应定理将热带计数与代数计数联系起来。

Result: 通过热带枚举与亏格1对应定理的结合，成功给出了Pandharipande代数枚举的另一种证明。

Conclusion: 热带几何方法可以作为代数枚举问题的一种有效验证或替代工具，本文结果进一步加深了热带-代数对应理论在亏格1情形下的理解。

Abstract: We tropically enumerate planar well-spaced elliptic curves with a fixed $j$-invariant. Combined with the recent genus 1 correspondence theorem of Cela--Koyama, this yields an alternative proof of Pandharipande's algebraic enumeration.

</details>


### [96] [Equivalences via twisted hyperholomorphic sheaves from transverse Lagrangian fibrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13403)
*Moritz Hartlieb,Saket Shah*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文利用Lagrangian纤维化构造了K3^[n]型和OG10型超Kähler流形乘积上的扭曲超全纯层，并由此证明了OG10型超Kähler流形的Lefschetz标准猜想和D-等价猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超Kähler流形是代数几何中的重要研究对象，Lefschetz标准猜想和Kuznetsov提出的D-等价猜想是其中的核心公开问题。本文借鉴Kapustka-Kapustka的思想，旨在通过Lagrangian纤维化的几何工具来构造所需的扭曲超全纯层，从而证明这些猜想在OG10型超Kähler流形上的成立。

Method: 本文采用Kapustka-Kapustka的方法，利用Lagrangian纤维化在K3^[n]型和OG10型超Kähler流形的乘积上构造扭曲超全纯层（twisted hyperholomorphic sheaves），以此为桥梁建立傅里叶-向井型变换，进而证明Lefschetz标准猜想和D-等价猜想。

Result: 成功证明了OG10型超Kähler流形上的Lefschetz标准猜想和D-等价猜想。

Conclusion: 本研究为超Kähler流形上的重要猜想提供了新的证明路径，表明Lagrangian纤维化与扭曲超全纯层的结合是研究超Kähler流形上经典猜想的有力工具。

Abstract: Following ideas of Kapustka-Kapustka, we use Lagrangian fibrations to construct twisted hyperholomorphic sheaves on products of hyperkähler manifolds of K3$^{[n]}$- and OG10-type. As applications, we prove the Lefschetz standard conjecture and the D-equivalence conjecture for hyperkähler manifolds of OG10-type.

</details>


### [97] [Sites and Grothendieck Topologies, Sites and Sheaves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13405)
*Arvid Siqveland*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了理解Grothendieck topos定义与应用所需的最少范畴论知识，阐述了site和sheaf的基本性质，并给出了在模空间理论中的应用。作者证明对于满足特定性质的范畴C，可以构造一个真范畴（而非stacks情况下的2-范畴）的对象scheme范畴。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在降低理解Grothendieck topos所需范畴论的入门门槛，同时解决stacks作为2-范畴在对象scheme构造中的复杂性，寻求一个更简洁的真范畴（proper category）构造方案。

Method: 首先给出Grothendieck topos定义所需的极小范畴论基础，然后阐述site和sheaf的基本性质，接着将这些工具应用于模空间理论，最后证明对于满足特定条件的范畴C，可以构造一个真范畴的对象scheme范畴，避免stacks中出现的2-范畴结构。

Result: 成功证明了对于具有特定性质的范畴C，可构造一个真范畴（proper category）而非2-范畴的scheme of objects范畴，并给出了Grothendieck topos在模空间理论中的应用。

Conclusion: 通过最少必需范畴论工具即可理解Grothendieck topos，并且在某些条件下可避免stacks的2-范畴复杂性，直接构造真范畴形式的对象scheme范畴。

Abstract: We give the minimum of category theory necessary for understanding the definition and applications of Grothendieck topos. We state the basic properties of sites and sheaves, and give applications to the theory of moduli. We prove that for categories $\mathbf C$ with explicit stated properties, we can construct a category of schemes of objects in $\mathbf C$ which is a proper category rather than a $2$-category as in the case of stacks.

</details>

### [98] [Rigidity theorems for cone structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12907)
*Tymon Frelik,Wojciech Kryński*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究微分流形上的锥结构（cone structures），特别是由常微分方程通过典范构造导出的锥结构。作者证明：以n维射影空间中的一般曲线或三维射影空间中的直纹面为模型的等平凡（isotrivial）锥结构必定是平坦的，并讨论了该方法在四维因果几何和无色散Lax系统中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 锥结构是切丛中的锥场，在微分几何、PDE理论和因果几何中具有重要地位。理解等平凡锥结构（即每点投影化锥射影等价于固定子流形）何时为平坦的，是几何分析中的基本问题，且对四维因果几何和色散less Lax系统等物理背景有直接关联。

Method: 论文考虑由常微分方程通过典范构造产生的锥结构，针对以n维射影空间中一般曲线和三维射影空间中直纹面为模型的等平凡锥结构，证明其平坦性。证明方法涉及将等平凡条件与ODE所对应的几何不变量（如射影二阶结构、韦尔张量等）相联系，利用模型子流形的代数几何性质推导约束条件，最终得出平坦性结论。

Result: 证明了：在由ODE典范构造产生的锥结构类中，(1) 以n维射影空间中一般曲线为模型的等平凡锥结构是平坦的；(2) 以三维射影空间中直纹面为模型的等平凡锥结构也是平坦的。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了等平凡锥结构的平坦性定理，为四维因果几何和色散less Lax系统的分类问题提供了新的几何工具，表明在特定模型类中，等平凡性条件会强制几何的平坦性。

Abstract: Cone structures on differentiable manifolds are fields of cones in the tangent bundle. A cone structure is isotrivial, modeled on a fixed immersed submanifold of the projective space, if at each point the projectivised cone is projectively equivalent to that submanifold. We consider cone structures arising from ordinary differential equations via a canonical construction. We prove that isotrivial cone structures in this class, modeled on generic curves in the $n$-dimensional projective space or ruled surfaces in the three-dimensional projective space, are flat. We also discuss applications to causal geometries in four dimensions and dispersionless Lax systems.

</details>


### [99] [Rigidity of stable spacelike capillary hypersurfaces in de Sitter and Minkowski spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13115)
*Hui Ma,Jiaxu Ma,Mingxuan Yang*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文证明了紧致类光毛细超曲面在de Sitter和Minkowski空间中的刚性定理：当支撑面为具有非负内曲率的类光全脐超曲面时，保体积稳定性蕴含全脐性。通过光锥模型构造切向共形Killing场，推导出适用于所有Lorentzian空间形式的统一Minkowski型公式，并利用有限迹恒等式结合非正Dirichlet Green修正来检测脐性缺陷，证明任何非全脐超曲面都存在使第二变分为正的容许测试函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在研究Lorentzian空间形式中紧致类光毛细超曲面的稳定性与全脐性之间的内在联系，推广经典刚性结果到类光支撑面的情形，并建立统一的变分分析框架。

Method: 采用光锥模型构造切向于支撑面的共形Killing场，推导统一的Minkowski型公式；利用线性化毛细Robin边界条件下的非齐次Jacobi方程及有限维测试函数族，结合有限迹恒等式（de Sitter情形辅以非正Dirichlet Green修正项）来分析第二变分的符号。

Result: /获得以下结果：当支撑为具有非负内曲率的类光全脐超曲面时，保体积稳定超曲面必为全脐的；任何非全脐超曲面都存在使第二变分为正的容许测试函数。对于负内曲率的支撑面，存在唯一的类时参数方向使得有限迹不再符号确定。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了类光毛细超曲面的刚性定理，其构造方法具有一般性——不仅统一了不同Lorentzian空间形式的情形，还能延伸到负内曲率支撑面，揭示了内曲率符号对变分稳定性行为的本质影响。

Abstract: We prove a rigidity theorem for compact spacelike capillary hypersurfaces in de~Sitter and Minkowski spaces: volume-preserving stability forces total umbilicity when the support is a spacelike totally umbilical hypersurface of nonnegative intrinsic curvature. Using the light-cone model, we construct conformal Killing fields tangent to the support and derive a unified Minkowski-type formula valid in all Lorentzian space forms. The resulting mean-zero functions satisfy an inhomogeneous Jacobi equation and the linearized capillary Robin boundary condition, and form canonical finite-dimensional test families. A finite-trace identity, supplemented in the de~Sitter cases by a nonpositive Dirichlet Green correction, detects the umbilicity defect \(n|h|^2-H^2\) with a definite sign; hence, every non-totally-umbilical hypersurface admits an admissible test function with positive second variation. The construction extends to supports of negative intrinsic curvature, where a unique timelike parameter direction prevents the finite trace from being sign-definite.

</details>


### [100] [Bismut-Torsion-Parallel Hermitian Manifolds With Constant Chern Holomorphic Sectional Curvature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13386)
*Haohao Wang*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文证明了任意复维数下具有平衡的Bismut平行挠率的紧致Hermitian流形，其Chern全纯截面曲率为非零常数时必为Kähler流形，从而在更广的类别中推进了这一著名的复几何猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复几何中的一个著名猜想断言：紧致Hermitian流形的Chern全纯截面曲率为非零常数时必为Kähler，为零常数时必为Chern平坦。该猜想已在复二维及若干特殊类别中得到证明，但对BTP（Bismut平行挠率）Hermitian流形，此前仅覆盖了非平衡情形、平衡三维及平衡四维情形，更高维度的平衡情形仍然开放。

Method: 本文针对具有平衡的Bismut平行挠率（BTP）的Hermitian流形，利用BTP结构的特殊性质，结合Chern全纯截面曲率为常数约束下的结构分析，在任意复维数下证明了非零常数情形。

Result: 成功证明了对于任意复维数下的紧致平衡BTP Hermitian流形，若Chern全纯截面曲率为非零常数，则该流形必为Kähler流形。这一结果统一并推广了Chen-Zheng关于非平衡情形和平衡三维情形，以及Wang-Zheng关于平衡四维情形的先前结果。

Conclusion: 作为推论，一个紧致BTP Hermitian流形若具有非零常数的Chern全纯截面曲率，则其度量g必为Kähler度量，这为最终全面解决该猜想在BTP类别中的情形提供了关键一步。

Abstract: A well-known conjecture in complex geometry states that a compact Hermitian manifold with constant Chern holomorphic sectional curvature must be Kähler when the constant is nonzero and Chern flat when the constant is zero. The conjecture is known in complex dimension two and in several special classes in higher dimensions. For Hermitian metrics with Bismut-parallel torsion, the non-balanced case and the balanced threefold case were established by Chen--Zheng, while the balanced fourfold case was settled recently by Wang--Zheng. In this article, we prove the nonzero case for balanced Bismut-torsion-parallel Hermitian manifolds in every complex dimension. As a corollary, we confirm that a compact BTP Hermitian manifold with Chern holomorphic sectional curvature is a nonzero constant, then $g$ is Kähler.

</details>


### [101] [Morse index of Karcher saddle towers in $\mathbb{R}^2 \times \mathbb{S}^1(m)$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13451)
*David Wiygul*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究了Hermann Karcher构造的完备单周期极小曲面族Ξ_k的商曲面Ξ_{k,m}的Morse指标和零化度，具体证明了Ξ_{k,2}的Morse指标为4k-3、零化度为3，以及Ξ_{3,m}的Morse指标为6m-4⌊mα*⌋-3，其零化度在一般情形为3、在mα*为整数时为7。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 明确计算极小曲面的Morse指标和零化度是几何分析中的重要问题，对于理解此类曲面的稳定性、谱性质以及其在新奇极小曲面构造中的作用具有基础性意义。

Method: 通过考虑Ξ_k在平移m个基本周期下的商曲面Ξ_{k,m}，分别针对m=2（对所有k≥3）和k=3（对所有m≥1）两种子族进行谱分析，利用曲面的对称性和Jacobi算子的谱理论来确定Morse指标与零化度。

Result: 证明了Ξ_{k,2}对所有k≥3的Morse指标为4k-3、零化度为3；对于Ξ_{3,m}，存在α*∈(1/3,1/2)，使得其Morse指标为6m-4⌊mα*⌋-3，零化度在一般情形为3，而当mα*为整数时为零化度7。

Conclusion: 该结果给出了这两类子族极小曲面Morse指标和零化度的精确公式，揭示了其指标随参数k和m的线性增长规律，尤其是零化度出现的例外跳跃现象反映了曲面对称性的特殊退化。

Abstract: For each integer $k \geq 3$ Hermann Karcher identified a complete singly periodic minimal surface $Ξ_k$ (unique up to similarity) with $2k$ ends asymptotic to the union of $k$ planes intersecting equiangularly along a single line and with genus zero in the quotient by a fundamental translation. Writing $Ξ_{k,m}$ for the quotient of $Ξ_k$ by translation through $m \geq 1$ fundamental periods, we study the Morse index and nullity of the subfamilies $Ξ_{k,2}$ and $Ξ_{3,m}$. In the $m=2$ case we prove for all $k \geq 3$ that $Ξ_{k,2}$ has Morse index $4k-3$ and nullity $3$. In the $k=3$ case we prove that there exists a real number $α^* \in (1/3,1/2)$ such that for all $m \geq 1$ the Morse index of $Ξ_{3,m}$ is $6m- 4 \lfloor mα^* \rfloor - 3$ and its nullity is $3$ unless $mα^*$ is an integer, in which case its nullity is $7$.

</details>


### [102] [A negative Kähler-Einstein threefold with non-integrable infinitesimal Einstein deformations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13481)
*Ari Krishna*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文构造了一个光滑的、典范极化的三维紧凯勒流形（非双全纯等价于正维数簇的乘积），其归一化Kähler-Einstein度量存在不可积的无穷小Einstein形变。该切方向作为无穷小复形变也是不可积的。该例子中无穷小Einstein形变空间为8维实空间，其中可积方向构成6维实子空间，子空间外的所有方向均被阻碍。这回答了Dai、Wang和Wei在实6维中提出的广义版本问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回答Dai-Wang-Wei提出的关于Einstein度量无穷小形变可积性问题：是否存在紧Kähler-Einstein流形，其度量存在不可积的无穷小Einstein形变？已有工作（如Koiso在复曲面上）研究了6维情形，本文旨在构造12维（实维数）的三维复流形例子，推广并回答广义问题。

Method: 通过构造一个典范极化的光滑三维流形（不是簇的乘积），利用Kähler-Einstein度量的形变理论分析其无穷小Einstein形变空间。具体地，计算形变空间的实维数（8维），并分析其可积方向（由6维子空间构成），证明其余方向的形变被阻碍（不可积）。

Result: 构造出实维数12（复维数3）的紧Kähler-Einstein流形，其无穷小Einstein形变空间维数为8（实），可积方向仅为6维子空间，子空间外的所有方向均被阻碍。该例非双全纯等价于正维数簇的乘积，同时该非可积方向也作为无穷小复形变被阻碍。

Conclusion: 该构造回答了Dai-Wang-Wei广义版本的问题：在实维数12（更高维）情形下，确实存在不可积的无穷小Einstein形变，且明确描述了可积与不可积方向的结构。

Abstract: We construct a smooth canonically polarized threefold, not biholomorphic to a product of positive-dimensional varieties, whose normalized Kähler-Einstein metric admits a non-integrable infinitesimal Einstein deformation. The same tangent direction is non-integrable as an infinitesimal complex deformation. In fact, the space of infinitesimal Einstein deformations in our example has real dimension 8, its integrable directions form a real 6-dimensional subspace, and every direction outside that subspace is obstructed. This answers both parts of a suitably generalized version of a question posed by Dai, Wang, and Wei in real dimension 6.

</details>


### [103] [Heat kernel geometry and Gromov's volume growth conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13553)
*Jian Ge*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文肯定地回答了1986年Gromov提出的猜想：具有非负Ricci曲率和数量曲率至少为1的完备非紧n维黎曼流形，其体积增长满足Vol_g(B(p,R))≤C_n·R^{n-2}。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gromov于1986年提出的这个猜想涉及非负Ricci曲率流形在正数量曲率约束下的体积增长上界，是黎曼几何中的一个经典问题，长期悬而未决。

Method: 作者通过引入热核Fisher度量（heat-kernel Fisher metric）和Nash熵作为分析工具来解决该猜想。

Result: 成功证明了猜想成立，即存在仅依赖于维数n的常数C_n，使得该流形上的任意半径R测地球体积有界于C_n·R^{n-2}。

Conclusion: 该结果用热核Fisher度量和Nash熵的方法完整解决了Gromov 1986年的猜想，为非负Ricci曲率与正数量曲率条件下流形的体积增长提供了严格的几何不等式。

Abstract: In 1986, Gromov asked whether every complete noncompact $n$-dimensional Riemannian manifold with nonnegative Ricci curvature and scalar curvature at least one satisfies:
  \[
  \Vol_g (B(p, R))\le C_{n}R^{n-2}
  \]
  for all $p\in M$ and $R>0$. We answer this question affirmatively using the heat-kernel Fisher metric and Nash entropy.

</details>

# math.RT [[Back]](#toc)

### [104] [Categorification of the genus two DAHA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13278)
*Semeon Arthamonov,Ievgen Makedonskyi,Daniil Sarafannikov*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文构造了平坦退化D(2|1,α)当前代数的分级可积表示的导范畴上的三个导出自函子，并证明它们在Grothendieck群中的类正是亏格2的Macdonald算子。通过显式计算分级Ext配对，推导出亏格2 Macdonald算子的自伴性及亏格2 Macdonald多项式在特定斜双线性形式下的正交性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将量子可积系统（Macdonald算子）与表示论中的几何/代数结构（超李代数的导范畴）建立联系，从而利用表示论工具研究Macdonald算子的性质（如自伴性和多项式正交性）。

Method: 在平坦退化D(2|1,α)当前代数的分级可积表示的导范畴上构造三个导出自函子；计算其Grothendieck类以识别为亏格2 Macdonald算子；通过显式计算分级Ext配对来证明算子自伴性和多项式正交性。

Result: 成功证明了三个导出自函子的Grothendieck类即为亏格2 Macdonald算子；通过显式计算分级Ext配对，证明了亏格2 Macdonald算子的自伴性以及亏格2 Macdonald多项式在特定斜双线性形式下的正交性。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了导范畴上的导出自函子与亏格2 Macdonald理论之间的深层联系，为Macdonald算子的自伴性和 Macdonald多项式正交性提供了表示论层面的证明，推广了相关的量子群/仿射Hecke代数方法到亏格2情形。

Abstract: We construct three derived endofunctors on the derived category of graded integrable representations of the current algebra of a flat degeneration of $D(2|1,α)$, and prove that their classes in the Grothendieck group are the genus two Macdonald operators. Computing the graded $\operatorname{Ext}$ pairing explicitly, we deduce the self-adjointness of the genus two Macdonald operators and the orthogonality of genus $2$ Macdonald polynomials with respect to the certain skew-bilinear form.

</details>


### [105] [Nilpotent orbits of sl(n,R) admit no Gibbs state](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12608)
*Jérémie Pierard de Maujouy,Guillaume Neuttiens*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文证明了在SL(n,R)的幂零轨道上，当n>2时不存在任何Gibbs态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gibbs态是定义在Hamiltonian G-流形上的概率分布，由李代数g中的开集参数化，是系统在群G作用下自然的平衡态。需要研究特定的几何对象（SL(n,R)的幂零轨道）上是否允许存在这样的平衡态。

Method: 作者针对SL(n,R)的幂零轨道进行专门分析，通过考察李代数参数化开集与轨道结构的相容性，证明当n>2时这些轨道不满足Gibbs态存在的必要条件。

Result: 对于n>2的情况，SL(n,R)的所有幂零轨道上均不存在任何Gibbs态。

Conclusion: 该结果揭示了SL(n,R)幂零轨道在平衡态理论中的一个局限性：当维数超过2时，这些轨道无法承载由群作用自然诱导的Gibbs平衡态，这对理解该系统的统计力学性质具有重要意义。

Abstract: Gibbs states are probability distributions defined on Hamiltonian G-manifolds. They are parametrized by an open set of the Lie algebra g and arise as natural equilibrium states with regard to the action of G on the system. In this paper, we focus on nilpotent orbits of SL(n,R). We show that for n>2, those orbits do not admit any Gibbs state.

</details>


### [106] [Langlands Duality and Invariant Differential Operators: the Case SL(2n+1)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13400)
*V. K. Dobrev*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文探讨Langlands对偶性与不变微分算子之间的桥梁构建，聚焦于SL(2n+1)群的情形，此前已处理过SL(2n)群的情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Langlands纲领是数学研究中极具影响力的课题，而不变微分算子似乎与该纲领无关，但两者都深深植根于Harish-Chandra的半单Lie群表示理论。作者希望在这两个看似无关的主题之间建立联系。

Method: 作者此前已研究了SL(2n)群的情形，本文进一步扩展到SL(2n+1)群。尽管这两个群相似，但其表示理论存在较大差异，需要针对性地处理。

Result: 本文尚未在摘要中给出具体结果，主要是说明研究进展——已从SL(2n)情形推进到SL(2n+1)情形。

Conclusion: 通过在SL(2n+1)群上开展工作，作者逐步在Langlands对偶性与不变微分算子之间搭建桥梁。

Abstract: Recently we started building a bridge between two cases of Langlands duality. The latter is one of the most influential topics in mathematical research. It has many different appearances and influential subtopics. Yet there is a topic that until now seems unrelated to the Langlands program. That is the topic of invariant differential operators. That is strange since both items are deeply rooted in Harish-Chandra's representation theory of semisimple Lie groups.
  We started with the case of the group ~$SL(2n)$. In the present paper we deal with the group $SL(2n+1)$. The two mentioned groups are similar, but their representation theories is rather different.

</details>


### [107] [Minimal Nilpotent Orbits of type G2, F4 and E8](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12985)
*Boming Jia*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文证明对于类型为G₂、F₄、E₈的复单李代数，其极小幂零轨道的闭包不能同构于任意光滑拟仿射簇X的余切丛T*X的仿射化(T*X)^aff。该结论不假设同构是等变的或Poisson的，并同样适用于类型A₁和A₂。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究幂零轨道闭包在辛几何和代数几何中的分类问题。具体地，要确定哪些幂零轨道的闭包可以表示为某个光滑拟仿射簇的余切丛的仿射化，这是一个关于辛奇异性何时具有“余切丛结构”的基本问题。此前可能有等变或Poisson假设下的结果，本文旨在去掉这些假设，证明更强的非存在性结论。

Method: 对于类型G₂、F₄、E₈以及A₁、A₂的复单李代数，通过分析其极小幂零轨道闭包的性质（如奇异点结构、分辨性质等），证明不存在光滑拟仿射簇X使得该闭包同构于(T*X)^aff。关键点在于不依赖等变性或Poisson结构的假设，采用更一般的代数几何论证。

Result: 在类型G₂、F₄、E₈下，极小幂零轨道的闭包不同构于任何光滑拟仿射簇的余切丛的仿射化；同样的结论在类型A₁和A₂中也成立。

Conclusion: 这些例外类型（以及A₁、A₂）的极小幂零轨道闭包在更一般的（非等变、非Poisson）意义下也不具有余切丛仿射化的结构，进一步加深了对这些辛奇异性几何性质的刻画。

Abstract: Let $\mathfrak g$ be a complex simple Lie algebra of type $G_2$, $F_4$, or $E_8$. We prove that the closure $\overline{\mathcal O}_{\min}(\mathfrak g)$ of its minimal nilpotent orbit is not isomorphic to $(T^*X)^{\mathrm{aff}}$ for any smooth quasi-affine variety $X$. We do not assume that the isomorphism is equivariant or Poisson. We also prove the same statement in types $A_1$ and $A_2$.

</details>


### [108] [Absolutely flat algebras in tensor categories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13137)
*Kevin Coulembier,Alexander Sherman*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究了张量范畴（刚性阿贝尔幺半范畴）的ind-完备化中半单代数、阿廷单代数和阿廷绝对平坦代数之间的关系。主要结果表明，在中等增长的对称张量范畴中（若接受领域内主要猜想则为所有），限制到交换代数时这三类代数重合；同时给出反例说明一般情况下这些概念会分化，不同于域上代数的经典情形。两个附录分别给出有限维态射空间但对象具有无限长度的张量范畴首例，以及Deligne定理（坦纳克范畴在代数闭域上是中性的）的推广。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在经典代数中，域上的半单代数、阿廷单代数和阿廷绝对平坦代数在交换情形下是一致的。本文试图考察这一经典结论在更一般的张量范畴（刚性阿贝尔幺半范畴）的ind-完备化中是否仍然成立，同时探讨这些代数的概念在何种条件下趋于一致、何种条件下发生分化。

Method: 论文采用范畴论与代数结构分析相结合的方法：在张量范畴的ind-完备化框架下系统比较三类代数的定义与性质；借助中等增长对称张量范畴的结构定理（依赖领域内主要猜想）来处理交换代数的情形；构造具体反例演示一般情况下概念的差异；附录中利用Deligne定理的推广来处理坦纳克范畴的中性化问题。

Result: 主要结果：在中等增长的对称张量范畴中（接受主要猜想则对所有对称张量范畴成立），交换代数意义上半单代数、阿廷单代数与阿廷绝对平坦代数三者重合。同时构造反例表明在一般张量范畴的ind-完备化中，这些概念不再等价。此外给出了一个新的张量范畴例子（有限维态射空间但对象无限长），以及Deligne定理在更一般框架下的推广。

Conclusion: 经典的"半单=阿廷单=阿廷绝对平坦"（对交换代数）在张量范畴的ind-完备化中并非普遍成立，仅在中等增长对称张量范畴的交换代数限制下恢复这种一致性。这表明张量范畴中的代数理论比域上代数的经典理论具有更丰富的结构层级，也说明相关的主要猜想（若成立）将使得这一经典结论在对所有对称张量范畴中成立。

Abstract: We study the relation between semisimple algebras, artinian simple algebras, and artinian absolutely flat algebras in ind-completions of tensor categories (rigid abelian monoidal categories). Our main results show that the three types of algebras coincide, if we restrict to commutative algebras in certain (all, if we accept the main conjectures in the field) symmetric tensor categories of moderate growth. We also provide examples to show that in general these notions tend to diverge, contrary to the classical case of algebras over a field. In one appendix we give the first, to the best of our knowledge, example of a tensor category with finite-dimensional morphism spaces but objects of infinite length. In a second appendix we establish a generalisation of Deligne's theorem that tannakian categories are neutral over algebraically closed fields.

</details>


### [109] [$g$-vector fans and picture categories for 0-Auslander extriangulated categories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13175)
*Erlend D. Børve,Maximilian Kaipel*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文将g-向量扇推广到带有投射silting对象的Hom-finite Krull-Schmidt 0-Auslander k-线性extriangulated范畴上，证明其具有由厚子范畴诱导的容许分割，从而定义了该范畴的picture category，并建立了厚子范畴与τ-垂直子范畴之间的双射，统一了之前所有picture category与τ-cluster morphism category的构造，同时引入分割扇的态射作为不同代数/范畴间picture category函子关系的统一框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在将g-向量扇和picture category的构造从有限维代数推广到更一般的0-Auslander extriangulated范畴，并统一此前分散在不同文献中的picture category、τ-cluster morphism category等构造，构建一个统一的框架来理解不同代数与范畴之间这些结构的函子关系。

Method: 该文在Hom-finite Krull-Schmidt 0-Auslander k-线性extriangulated范畴上，利用投射silting对象定义g-向量扇；借助厚子范畴给出g-向量扇的容许分割；通过建立由presilting对象生成的厚子范畴（包含所有投射-内射对象）与T的自同态代数的τ-垂直子范畴之间的双射，定义picture category；并引入分割扇态射的概念来统一刻画不同对象之间的函子关系。

Result: 在0-Auslander extriangulated范畴上建立了g-向量扇及其容许分割，定义了picture category；证明了厚子范畴与τ-垂直子范畴间的双射定理；表明该构造统一了有限维代数上所有已知的picture category和τ-cluster morphism category的构造；引入了分割扇态射以提供统一框架。

Conclusion: 该工作将picture category理论推广到更广泛的0-Auslander extriangulated范畴框架中，建立了厚子范畴与τ-垂直子范畴之间的双射，并通过分割扇态射提供了一个统一的函子性框架，使得不同代数和范畴之间的picture category关系得以系统性地描述。

Abstract: We extend the notion of $\mathbf{g}$-vector fan so that it is defined for a Hom-finite Krull-Schmidt 0-Auslander $k$-linear extriangulated category $\mathcal{C}$ with a projective silting object $T$. Moreover, we show that the $\mathbf{g}$-vector fan admits an admissible partition, in the sense of the second-named author, which is induced by thick subcategories. One can thus define the picture category of $\mathcal{C}$. We establish a bijection between thick subcategories of $\mathcal{C}$ generated by presilting objects containing all projective-injective objects and $τ$-perpendicular subcategories of the endomorphism $k$-algebra of $T$. This shows that our construction unifies all previous constructions of picture categories and $τ$-cluster morphism categories of finite-dimensional algebras. We introduce morphisms of partitioned fans to provide a common framework for the functorial relationships between picture categories of different algebras and categories.

</details>


### [110] [Homogeneous Weights on Semigroup Algebras of Finite Commutative Semigroups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13180)
*M. H. Shahzamanian*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究了有限域上扭曲收缩半群代数的行列式理论，将Steinberg的行列式理论推广到扭曲收缩半群代数上，建立了Frobenius判据，并为双生成扭曲收缩幺半群代数发展了结构理论。研究发现某些类别的行列式仅取决于其零-非零模式，与余循环值无关，因此只需验证平凡特征即可。在此基础上构造了Frobenius扭曲收缩半群代数的齐次权重，并刻画了满足平均性质的主理想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限半群的行列式与其半群代数的Frobenius性质密切相关，并在编码理论中有重要应用。现有Steinberg行列式理论针对的是普通收缩半群代数，尚未推广到扭曲（twisted）情形。此外，Frobenius环上齐次权重的构造依赖于Frobenius性质，扭曲收缩半群代数场合下的齐次权重构造与非Frobenius情形的处理尚属空白。

Method: 本文采用代数方法，主要包括：(1) 将Steinberg行列式理论推广至扭曲收缩半群代数，建立Frobenius判据；(2) 发展双生成扭曲收缩幺半群代数的结构理论，利用定义恒等式对半群进行刻画；(3) 证明在特定类别下矩阵行列式仅依赖于零-非零模式，从而与余循环值无关；(4) 借助特征论构造齐次权重，并推广经典齐次权重概念到指定主理想族上。

Result: 建立了扭曲收缩半群代数的Frobenius判据；识别出若干类别使得行列式仅由零-非零模式决定，从而与余循环值无关，平凡特征的结果可推广至所有特征；在适当假设下这些半群可由少量定义恒等式完全确定；成功构造了Frobenius扭曲收缩半群代数的齐次权重，并对非Frobenius情形刻画了满足平均性质的主理想，将齐次权重概念推广到指定主理想族。

Conclusion: 本文成功将Steinberg行列式理论从普通收缩半群代数推广至扭曲情形，建立了相应的Frobenius判据，并揭示了行列式对余循环值的不敏感性。通过齐次权重的构造与推广，拓展了Frobenius环齐次权重的经典理论，为编码理论提供了新的代数工具。

Abstract: The determinant of a finite semigroup is closely related to the Frobenius property of its corresponding semigroup algebra, and it plays a significant role in coding theory applications. In this paper, we study determinants of twisted contracted semigroup algebras over finite fields. We extend Steinberg's determinant theory to this setting by establishing a Frobenius criterion for twisted contracted semigroup algebras over finite fields. We also develop a structural theory for two-generated twisted contracted monoid algebras. In particular, we identify classes for which the determinant of the associated matrix depends only on its zero--nonzero pattern, and is therefore independent of the values of the cocycle. Consequently, it suffices to verify the determinant condition for the trivial character, from which the corresponding result for all characters follows. Under suitable assumptions, these semigroups are completely determined by a small set of defining identities. Motivated by the character-theoretic construction of homogeneous weights on finite Frobenius rings, we construct homogeneous weights for Frobenius twisted contracted semigroup algebras and investigate the non-Frobenius case. In particular, we characterize the principal ideals on which the character-average weight satisfies the averaging property of homogeneous weights and thereby extend the classical notion of homogeneous weight to a prescribed family of principal ideals.

</details>


### [111] [Moduli of super-representations of quivers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13375)
*Sanjay Amrutiya,Umesh Dubey*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 该论文通过将King的几何不变量理论（GIT）框架推广到超设置，构造了超表示箭图的模空间，并进一步构造了框架箭图（无需加倍）的超箭图簇，同时在超Grassmannian和超旗簇中实现了几何商。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的King GIT框架已成功用于构造普通箭图表示的空间，但超表示（super-representations）的模空间尚未系统建立。本文旨在填补这一空白，为超表示箭图建立严格的模空间理论。

Method: 方法包括：1）将King的GIT框架推广到超设置；2）利用超一般线性群的偶部以及一个奇偶平移算子（parity-shifting operator）来构造模空间；3）该模空间在拓扑意义上参数化固定超维数的超表示；4）对带框架的箭图（无需加倍构造）给出超箭图簇的构造方案；5）将超Grassmannian和超旗簇实现为上述框架中的几何商。

Result: 成功构造了超表示箭图的模空间，建立了超箭图簇的构造框架，并将超Grassmannian和超旗簇统一纳入该几何商框架中。

Conclusion: 该工作将King的GIT方法系统性地推广至超设置，为超表示的模空间理论提供了基础框架，并展示了其在超几何对象（如超Grassmannian、超旗簇）中的广泛应用。

Abstract: In this note, we construct moduli spaces of super-representations of quivers by extending King's Geometric Invariant Theory (GIT) framework to the super setting. Using the even part of super general linear groups and a parity-shifting operator, we construct moduli spaces that topologically parameterize super-representations of a fixed super-dimension.
  We also outline the construction of super quiver varieties for super-representations of framed quivers (without doubling); and realise super-Grassmannians and super-flag varieties as geometric quotients within this setting.

</details>


<div id='gr-qc'></div>
