### [135] [Dark matter in scale-invariant gravity with hidden-sector condensation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12456)
*Juan P. Garcés,Jisuke Kubo,Manfred Lindner,Markus Reinig*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一框架，基于经典尺度不变的二次引力理论（R²引力）与强相互作用隐藏扇区的耦合，将电弱尺度起源、宇宙暴胀和暗物质这三个看似独立的问题统一解释。隐藏扇区的禁闭动态产生普朗克尺度并触发电弱对称性破缺，R²标量度通过引力冻结产生机制同时为可见和隐藏扇区再加热，并研究三种暗物质候选方案的丰度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电弱尺度起源、宇宙暴胀和暗物质通常被视为标准模型之外相互独立的问题。作者试图探索是否可能由一个统一的理论框架同时解释这三个现象，即基于经典尺度不变的二次引力与强耦合隐藏扇区之间的共同底层框架。

Method: 采用经典尺度不变的二次引力（包含R²项），其中R²项天然实现Starobinsky暴胀；隐藏扇区的禁闭动态生成普朗克尺度并通过引力诱导的Higgs质量产生机制触发电弱对称性破缺。R²伴随的标量自由度通过普适的能量-动量张量耦合对可见和隐藏扇区进行再加热，通过引力冻结产生机制产生隐藏扇区态。具体考察三种隐藏扇区实现：隐藏η'介子、隐藏矢量玻色子、带电隐藏π介子作为暗物质候选。

Result: 针对三种隐藏扇区实现（隐藏η'介子、隐藏矢量玻色子、带电隐藏π介子），推导了相应的暗物质和暗辐射遗迹丰度，并给出了对应的参数空间约束。

Conclusion: 作者表明电弱尺度、暴胀和暗物质可以来自一个共同的统一框架，该框架基于经典尺度不变的R²引力与强耦合隐藏扇区，为理解标准模型之外的这三重难题提供了统一的唯象学路径。

Abstract: The origin of the electroweak scale, cosmic inflation, and dark matter are often treated as independent problems beyond the Standard Models of particle physics and cosmology. In this work, we explore the possibility that they instead arise from a common underlying framework based on classically scale-invariant quadratic gravity coupled to a strongly interacting hidden sector. The $R^2$ term naturally realizes Starobinsky inflation, while confinement in the hidden sector dynamically generates the Planck scale and triggers electroweak symmetry breaking through a gravitationally induced Higgs mass generation mechanism. The scalar degree of freedom associated with the $R^2$ term subsequently reheats both the visible and hidden sectors through universal couplings to the energy-momentum tensor, leading to the gravitational freeze-in production of hidden-sector states. We investigate three representative realizations of the hidden sector in which the dark matter candidate is either a hidden $η'$ meson, a hidden vector boson, or charged hidden pions, and derive the corresponding dark matter and dark radiation relic abundances.

</details>


### [136] [Probing CP-violating top-quark dipole moments with tomographic observables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12463)
*Maxim Perelstein,Minal Shaik,Taewook Youn*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于量子断层扫描的顶夸克对自旋密度矩阵重建方法，从双轻子角关联中提取对CP破坏敏感的可观测物理量，并构造最优可观测量，在LHC和FCC-ee上对(色)电偶极矩算符的灵敏度进行了系统研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 顶夸克耦合中的CP破坏是超越标准模型新物理的重要信号，但传统实验可观测量对这些算符的灵敏度有限。作者希望通过量子断层扫描方法，利用对称性参数识别对CP破坏敏感的自旋关联可观测量，从而提升对新物理标度的探测能力。

Method: 采用量子断层扫描程序从顶夸克对双轻子衰变道的角关联中重建完整自旋密度矩阵；利用对称性论证识别对CP破坏敏感的可观测物理量；构造结合断层扫描与运动学信息的最优可观测量，实现统计最优灵敏度；基于快速探测器模拟对顶夸克对产生和衰变进行分析。

Result: 在95%置信水平下，FCC-ee（365 GeV, 3 ab^-1）对电弱电偶极矩的新物理标度灵敏度为10-15 TeV；LHC Run 2和HL-LHC对顶夸克色电偶极矩的灵敏度分别为40 TeV和200 TeV，均超过传统实验可观测量。

Conclusion: 量子断层扫描方法在搜索CP破坏方面显示出显著优势，其灵敏度超越实验当前使用的传统可观测量，证明了这种方法的强大潜力。

Abstract: Quantum tomography program reconstructs the full spin density matrix of top-quark pairs from dilepton angular correlations at both electron-positron and hadron colliders. We use symmetry arguments to identify tomographic observables sensitive to CP violation in top quark couplings. As a concrete example, we study the sensitivity of these observables to (chromo-)electric dipole moment operators at the LHC and the FCC-ee. We also construct the optimal observable, which combines tomographic and kinematic information to achieve statistically optimal sensitivity for each operator and production process. The analysis is based on fast detector simulation of top pair production and decay in the dilepton channel. We project a $95\%$ CL sensitivity to new-physics scales (the inverse dipole couplings) of $10-15$ TeV for the electroweak electric dipoles at the FCC-ee ($365$ GeV, $3~{\rm ab}^{-1}$) and of 40 (200) TeV for the top chromo-electric dipole moment at the LHC Run 2 (HL-LHC). This sensitivities exceed those of the traditional observables currently used by experiments to use for these operators, indicating the power of the tomographic approach to search for CP violation.

</details>


### [137] [Soft Collinear Effective Theory for Heavy QCD Axions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12467)
*Deepanshu Bisht,Sabyasachi Chakraborty,Siddhartha Karmakar,Atanu Samanta*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文发展了一个用于重QCD轴子的软共线有效理论(SCET)框架，以B→Ka为基准过程，研究了两种低能实现下的轴子产生机制，识别了谱线散射贡献在何种条件下对重轴子产生变得重要，并推导了相应的轴子衰变常数f_a的约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重QCD轴子是超出标准模型的重要候选者，但对其在强子衰变中的产生机制尚缺乏完整的有效理论描述。特别是，谱线散射贡献相对于软重叠贡献的相对重要性此前未被系统研究，需要一个统一的因子化框架来正确计算不同实现下的B→Ka振幅，并用于约束轴子参数空间。

Method: 作者采用SCET框架，考虑两种低能实现：(1)在μ~m_b尺度仅假设aGĞ为独立轴子相互作用，通过在弱有效理论中消除冗余的味改变导数-胶子算子，得到新维数为7的轴子算子O∂ag，将其匹配到SCET并推导出领头阶软贡献和谱线散射贡献，后者用微扰硬核以及B介子和K介子的光锥分布振幅因子化表示；(2)假设aGĞ的Wilson系数在电弱尺度以上存在，通过重整化群演化与匹配诱导出直接的b→sa算子。

Result: 在第一种实现中，谱线散射贡献在幂次展开中与软重叠项同阶，约占软贡献的25%。在第二种实现中，直接b→sa算子导致软形状因子贡献占主导，而胶子谱线散射项数值上次领头（约6-7%）。两种实现下均推导了对轴子衰变常数f_a的约束并比较了其唯象后果。

Conclusion: 作者识别出了谱线散射在稀有B介子衰变中重轴子产生变得关键的具体条件：只有当aGĞ是低能尺度下唯一的独立轴子相互作用时（即第一种实现），谱线散射贡献才具有显著地位；若b→sa直接算子存在（第二种实现），则软成形因子贡献占据主导。这一判别对实验搜寻重轴子及约束f_a具有重要意义。

Abstract: We develop a soft-collinear effective theory (SCET) framework for heavy QCD axion, considering two low-energy realizations and taking $B\to Ka$ as a benchmark mode. In the first realization, $aG\widetilde G$ is assumed to be the only independent axion interaction at the scale $μ\sim m_b$. We show that eliminating the redundant flavor-changing derivative-gluon operator in the weak effective theory generates a new dimension-seven axion operator identified as $\mathrm{O}_{\partial ag}$. We match this operator onto SCET and derive the corresponding leading-power soft and spectator-scattering contributions to $B\to Ka$. We obtain a factorized expression for the spectator contribution in terms of perturbative hard kernels and the $B$- and $K$-meson light-cone distribution amplitudes. The spectator contribution arises at the same order in the power expansion as the soft-overlap term and amounts to approximately $25\%$ of the soft contribution. In the second realization, the Wilson coefficient of $aG\widetilde G$ is assumed to be present above the electroweak scale. Renormalization-group evolution and matching then induce a direct $b\to sa$ operator, which subsequently results in a dominant soft form-factor contribution, whereas the gluonic spectator term turns out to be numerically subleading ($\sim 6-7\%$). We thus identify the conditions under which spectator scattering becomes relevant for heavy-axion production in rare $B$-meson decays. Finally, we derive the corresponding bounds on the axion decay constant $f_a$ for both realizations and compare their phenomenological implications.

</details>


### [138] [Defect-Mediated Conversion: Dark Matter from Cosmological Domain-Wall Scattering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12468)
*João Paulo Pinheiro,Mohamed Younes Sassi*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的暗物质产生机制——缺陷介导转换（DMC）：宇宙学畴壁核心中的标量凝聚体作为标准模型与暗扇区之间的混合门户，热浴费米子穿过畴壁时被部分且非热地转换为暗物质粒子。转换概率由单个无量纲混合面积决定，产量由网络的面积密度决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的暗物质产生机制（如热冻结、freeze-in）在解释某些暗物质质量区间和耦合参数空间时存在困难，尤其是在暗粒子质量超过浴温度和媒介子质量的情况下。作者旨在提出一种新的、非热学的暗物质产生机制，利用宇宙学畴壁的拓扑结构及其核心的标量凝聚体作为产生暗物质的门户，以扩展暗物质产生的可行参数空间。

Method: 作者提出缺陷介导转换（DMC）机制。具体而言：宇宙学畴壁核心存在标量凝聚体，充当标准模型与暗扇区之间的混合门户；当热浴费米子穿越畴壁时，它们被部分地、非热地转换为暗粒子。转换概率由畴壁轮廓构建的单一无量纲混合面积决定，而暗物质产额由畴壁网络的面积密度决定。对于轻暗粒子，转换概率独立于入射能量和暗物质质量，遗迹丰度塌缩为一条从keV Lyman-α下限到畴壁形成尺度的单一质量-耦合关系；对于重暗粒子，转换将单个入射费米子的能量转化为暗物质质量，从而可以填充质量超过浴温度和媒介子质量的暗扇区。

Result: 同一门户也能产生普通的freeze-in暗物质，但DMC机制受益于相干畴壁面积增强，在固定耦合下比freeze-in主导一到两个数量级，并且在freeze-in失效的重暗物质体系中仍然有效。该机制能够产生从keV到远超媒介子质量范围的暗物质，扩展了可行的参数空间。

Conclusion: DMC提供了一种新的、普适的暗物质产生途径，利用宇宙学畴壁的几何增强效应，可以在轻暗物质和重暗物质两个区间均有效产生暗物质，且相比传统freeze-in在固定耦合下有数量级优势。这为暗物质搜索和宇宙学模型提供了新的理论框架和实验可检验的预测。

Abstract: We propose a new mechanism for dark matter genesis, \emph{Defect-Mediated Conversion} (DMC). Cosmological domain walls can host a scalar condensate in their core acting as a mixing portal between the Standard Model and a dark sector: thermal-bath fermions crossing the wall are partially and non-thermally converted into dark particles. The conversion probability is governed by a single dimensionless mixing area built from the wall profile, and the yield by the area density of the network. For a light dark particle the probability is independent of both the incident energy and the dark mass, and the relic abundance collapses onto a single mass--coupling relation running from the keV Lyman-$α$ floor to the wall-formation scale. For a heavy one, conversion turns the energy of a single incident fermion into the dark mass, populating dark sectors with masses exceeding both the bath temperature and the mediator mass. The same portal also sources ordinary freeze-in, but DMC, boosted by the coherent wall-area enhancement, dominates it by one to two orders of magnitude at fixed couplings and remains operative in the heavy regime, where freeze-in shuts off.

</details>


### [139] [Singlet-doublet dark matter beyond freeze-out](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12480)
*Prudhvi N. Bhattiprolu,Aaron Pierce,Alexander Takla*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该论文系统分析了Singlet-Doublet费米子暗物质模型在弱标度下的暗物质丰度产生机制，重点研究了小Yukawa耦合极限下的宇宙学与对撞机物理特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Singlet-Doublet费米子暗物质模型是一个经济且可行的弱标度暗物质模型，其暗物质丰度可通过多种不同机制产生（包括freeze-out、co-scattering、freeze-in和SuperWIMP），但不同参数区域（特别是小Yukawa耦合极限）下的产生机制和物理可观测性尚未被充分研究。

Method: 作者系统地分析了该模型在不同Yukawa耦合强度下实现正确暗物质丰度的各种途径，特别关注小Yukawa耦合极限。在该极限下，传统直接探测和间接探测信号非常微弱，因此着重探讨了对撞机物理中可能出现的丰富信号。

Result: 该模型可以通过四种不同的机制（freeze-out含共湮灭、co-scattering、freeze-in、SuperWIMP）产生正确的暗物质剩余丰度。在小Yukawa耦合区域，虽然直接和间接探测信号难以观测，但模型展现出了丰富的对撞机物理现象学。

Conclusion: 小Yukawa耦合的Singlet-Doublet费米子暗物质模型为对撞机实验提供了独特的探针，尽管天文学探测手段在此时失效，但高能对撞机成为测试该参数区域的主要途径。

Abstract: The Singlet-Doublet fermion model of dark matter is an economical weak-scale dark matter model that realizes the dark matter abundance through interactions with the electroweak bosons of the Standard Model. Depending on the size of the Yukawa couplings in the model, the dark matter relic abundance can be produced via freeze-out (including co-annihilation), co-scattering, freeze-in, or the SuperWIMP mechanism. We analyze the ways this model can realize the dark matter density with emphasis on the small Yukawa coupling regime. In this limit, direct and indirect detection are difficult, but a rich collider phenomenology is possible.

</details>


### [140] [Singlet-Doublet fermion origin of dark matter, neutrino mass and inverse first-order electroweak phase transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12483)
*Debasish Borah,Indrajit Saha,Sujit Kumar Sahoo,Narendra Sahu,Shashwat Sharma*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了Scotogenic类型辐射中微子质量模型中逆一阶电弱相变（IFOEWPT）与可观测引力波的可能性。模型扩展了标准模型，引入两代SU(2)L单态-双重态费米子、一个标量单态和三个右手中微子。较轻的单态-双重态费米子在中性组分在残余Z2对称性下作为暗物质候选，较重的费米子与Higgs强耦合导致宇宙经历两次一阶相变（IFOEWPT）。在满足中微子质量、暗物质遗迹密度和IFOEWPT要求并纳入实验约束后，最终允许的参数空间可在对撞机、直接探测、引力波和CMB实验中检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索同时解释中微子质量、暗物质和引力波可观测性的统一理论框架。标准模型无法解释这些现象，而辐射中微子质量模型（Scotogenic型）为这些问题的联合解决提供了可能。特别是逆一阶电弱相变的引入为引力波信号提供了新的产生机制。

Method: 构建一个扩展标准模型的Scotogenic型辐射中微子质量模型，包含两代SU(2)L单态-双重态费米子、一个标量单态和三个右手中微子。右手中微子提供轻Dirac中微子的右手分量，单态-双重态费米子和标量单态促成单圈中微子质量图。利用较轻的单态-双重态费米子中性组分作为Z2对称性保护下的暗物质，较重费米子与Higgs的强耦合驱动IFOEWPT（宇宙经历两个不同的一阶相变从对称相到最终破缺相）。通过联合约束中微子质量、暗物质遗迹密度和IFOEWPT条件，并纳入现有实验约束来扫描参数空间。

Result: 得到了满足中微子质量、暗物质遗迹密度和IFOEWPT要求且符合现有实验约束的允许参数空间。该参数空间有望在未来的对撞机实验、直接探测实验、引力波探测和CMB实验中得到检验。

Conclusion: 该模型提供了一个同时解释中微子质量、暗物质和引力波信号的可行框架，其允许的参数空间对未来多信使实验具有明确的预言能力，值得在近未来的实验中进行探测验证。

Abstract: We study the possibility of an inverse first-order electroweak phase transition (IFOEWPT) and observable gravitational waves (GW) in a radiative neutrino mass model of scotogenic type where singlet-doublet (SD) fermions, the lightest of whom is the dark matter (DM) candidate, generate the necessary seesaw at one-loop level. Considering the possibility of light neutrinos being Dirac for simplicity and additional detection prospects, we extend the standard model (SM) with two generations of $SU(2)_L$ singlet and doublet fermions, one singlet scalar, and three right-handed neutrinos (RHNs). While RHNs provide the right chiral parts of light Dirac neutrinos, the SD fermions and the scalar singlet facilitate the one-loop neutrino mass diagram. The neutral component of the lighter SD fermion, stabilized under a residual $Z_2$ symmetry plays the role of DM while the heavier SD fermions strongly couple to the Higgs leading to an IFOEWPT where the Universe undergoes two different first-order phase transition as it goes from the symmetric to the final broken Higgs phase. We constrain the parameter space from the requirements of generating the correct neutrino mass, DM relic as well as IFOEWPT while incorporating the existing constraints from different experiments. The final allowed parameter space of the model can be probed at collider, direct-detection, GW and cosmic microwave background (CMB) experiments in near future.

</details>


### [141] [Spontaneous Scoto-leptogenesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12497)
*Arghyajit Datta,Hyun Min Lee,Jun-Ho Song*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种低尺度自发轻子生成机制（Spontaneous Scoto-leptogenesis），在动力学最小scotogenic模型中同时解释中微子质量和惰性标量暗物质。通过整体U(1)_{B-L}对称性破缺产生的滚动Majoron诱导B-L电荷的有效化学势，结合右手中微子的高效衰变与逆衰变，经电弱sphaleron过程产生观测到的重子不对称性。该机制在强冲刷区域有效，将最轻右手中微子的可行质量降至TeV尺度，并探讨了Majoron暗物质与多组分暗区的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的热scotogenic轻子生成机制需要右手中微子质量处于较高能标，难以被直接实验检验。作者旨在通过自发轻子生成机制降低右手中微子的可行质量标度至TeV范围，从而在统一框架中同时解释中微子质量、暗物质与重子不对称性的起源，并为直接探测、对撞机搜索等实验提供可检验的预言。

Method: 该机制利用全局U(1)_{B-L}对称性自发破缺产生的滚动Majoron作为驱动力，在存在B-L破坏相互作用（右手中微子衰变与逆衰变）的环境中诱导有效的B-L化学势，进而在强冲刷（strong washout）区域产生轻子不对称性，再通过电弱sphaleron转换为重子不对称性。作者同时分析了λ_5耦合在消除惰性标量不对称性中的作用，并研究了通过动力学错位（kinetic misalignment）机制实现Majoron暗物质的可能性，构建了包含惰性标量与Majoron的多组分暗区。

Result: 该机制在强冲刷区域有效运作，成功将最轻右手中微子的可行质量降低至TeV尺度，使具有两个层级右手中微子的热scotogenic轻子生成变得可被直接测试。研究明确了λ_5耦合在自发轻子生成和消除惰性标量不对称性中的关键作用，并展示了通过动力学错位实现Majoron暗物质的可能性，证实了包含惰性标量和Majoron的多组分暗区在该模型中可以成立。

Conclusion: 该框架统一解释了低尺度重子生成、中微子质量以及多组分暗区的起源，可通过直接探测实验、对撞机搜索惰性标量、以及未来对Majoron暗物质或暗辐射的探测等多种互补实验途径进行检验。

Abstract: We propose a low-scale spontaneous leptogenesis scenario within the dynamical minimal scotogenic model for accommodating neutrino masses and inert scalar dark matter simultaneously. Thus, we dub the mechanism Spontaneous Scoto-leptogenesis. In this setup, a rolling Majoron arising from the global $U(1)_{B-L}$ symmetry breaking induces an effective chemical potential for the $B-L$ charge in the presence of $B-L$ violating interactions that allow for the efficient decays and inverse decays of right handed neutrinos (RHN), so it gives rise to the observed baryon asymmetry of the Universe through the electroweak sphaleron conversion. The mechanism becomes effective in the strong washout regime and successfully lowers the viable mass scale of the lightest RHN to the range of TeV scales, thereby making the thermal scotogenic leptogenesis with two hierarchical RHNs accessible to direct tests. We identify the roles of the $λ_5$ coupling for spontaneous leptogenesis and inert scalar dark matter through the efficient erasure of the inert scalar asymmetry. We also explore the regime for Majoron dark matter from the kinetic misalignment, showing that a multicomponent dark sector comprising the inert scalar and the Majoron can be realized in the model. The resulting framework provides a unified origin for low-scale baryogenesis, neutrino masses, and multicomponent dark sector, so it can be tested by complementary experimental probes through direct detection experiments, collider searches for inert scalars, and future detection of Majoron dark matter or dark radiation.

</details>


### [142] [N$^3$LO soft-gluon corrections for $Zγ$ production](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12556)
*Nikolaos Kidonakis,Kaan Şimşek*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文计算了Zγ产生过程中单粒子包含（1PI）运动学下的软胶子修正，达到次次次领头阶（N³LO）精度，并给出近似N³LO（aN³LO）的QCD预言。在13 TeV和13.6 TeV的LHC对撞能量下计算了光子横动量p_T^γ和快度y^γ的微分截面，系统检查了各阶K因子，并在每个微扰阶量化了尺度与PDF不确定性。结果表明软胶子修正在高p_T区域显著，且aN³LO下观察到良好的微扰收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Zγ产生过程是对QCD、电弱相互作用及新物理搜索的重要探针。精确的微扰QCD预言需要更高阶的修正来提高理论精度，尤其是高横动量区域的软胶子修正对理论预言影响显著，需要在N³LO层次提供更精确的理论预测。

Method: 在单粒子包含（1PI）运动学框架下，通过计算Zγ产生的软胶子修正至N³LO精度，构建近似N³LO（aN³LO）的QCD理论预言。对光子横动量和快度的微分截面进行计算，并系统分析各阶K因子以及尺度不确定性和PDF不确定性的传播。

Result: 成功计算出Zγ产生的aN³LO微分散射截面，覆盖13 TeV和13.6 TeV两个LHC对撞能量。发现软胶子修正在高p_T区域效应显著，aN³LO下微扰级数收敛良好，各阶尺度与PDF不确定性均被系统量化。

Conclusion: aN³LO的软胶子修正在高p_T区域提供重要改进，具有良好的微扰收敛性，所得到的精确理论预言可作为LHC实验测量Zγ产生的可靠参考基准，有助于后续精确测量与新物理搜索。

Abstract: We calculate soft-gluon corrections for $Zγ$ production through next-to-next-to-next-to-leading-order (N$^3$LO) in one-particle-inclusive (1PI) kinematics. We use these results to produce approximate N$^3$LO (aN$^3$LO) QCD predictions for $Zγ$ production cross sections in proton-proton collisions at LHC energies. Differential cross sections in photon transverse momentum $p_T^γ$ and rapidity $y^γ$ are computed at 13 TeV and 13.6 TeV collider energies. Higher-order $K$-factors are examined systematically, and scale and PDF uncertainties are quantified at each perturbative order. Soft-gluon corrections are found to be significant in the high-$p_T$ region, with good perturbative convergence observed at aN$^3$LO.

</details>


### [143] [A Novel One-loop Model for Majorana Neutrino Mass and Dark Matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12646)
*Mohamed Belfkir,Mohamed Amin Loualidi,Salah Nasri*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次实现了有限单圈T4-3-i拓扑的场论构造，用于Majorana中微子质量的Weinberg算符实现。通过引入Dirac费米子和Majorana费米子并施加Z2对称性，避免了低阶I型/III型跷跷板贡献。作者系统分类了电弱荷分配，并详细研究了最小单-双态实现（T4-3-i-B1），包括中微子质量结构、振荡数据适配、轻子味破坏、电弱精密观测量、暗物质丰度与直接探测约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的一圈中微子质量图（T4-3-i拓扑）中，连接两个外腿轻子-希格斯对的费米子若为Majorana单态或三重态，会在树级产生I型或III型跷跷板贡献，使圈图不再是中微子质量的领先来源。作者旨在消除这种低阶贡献，实现一个纯一圈主导的中微子质量机制。

Method: 将连接外腿的介质费米子改为Dirac费米子N，把轻子数破坏放在圈内独立的Majorana费米子ψ上，并施加精确的Z2对称性使新标量惰性并稳定最轻奇数宇称态。作者分类了所有允许的电弱荷分配，聚焦于最小实现T4-3-i-B1（含一个Dirac费米子、一个Majorana费米子、一个惰性标量双重态和一个惰性标量单态），并对该模型进行了全面的唯象学分析。

Result: 该模型产生秩为2的中微子质量矩阵，预言一个无质量中微子。作者检验了正常和倒置中微子质量顺序，并考虑了中微子振荡、宇宙学数据、带电轻子味破坏（含μ-e转换）、电弱精密观测量、h→γγ、理论自洽条件、丰度及直接探测约束。费米子和标量暗物质均可行：费米子候选者仅有圈级希格斯耦合导致自旋无关散射被强烈压低，标量候选者通过树级希格斯portal耦合且可高于中微子floor而仍与当前极限兼容。两种情况下，与惰性标量的共湮灭对再现观测丰度至关重要。

Conclusion: T4-3-i-B1模型提供了一个自洽且可检验的一圈中微子质量机制，摆脱了低阶跷跷板干扰，同时为暗物质和中微子质量提供了统一的解释框架，并为实验验证提供了明确的唯象学预言。

Abstract: We present the first complete field-theoretic realization of the finite one-loop T4-3-i topology for Majorana neutrino mass. Here, T4-3-i denotes a one-loop realization of the Weinberg operator in which a fermion links the two external lepton--Higgs pairs. If this fermion is a Majorana singlet or triplet, the same interactions generate a tree-level type-I or type-III seesaw contribution, respectively, so that the loop is not the leading source of neutrino mass. This lower-order contribution is removed by taking the mediator to be a Dirac fermion $N$, placing lepton-number violation in a separate Majorana fermion $ψ$ inside the loop, and imposing an exact $Z_2$ symmetry that keeps the new scalars inert and stabilizes the lightest odd state. We classify the allowed electroweak charge assignments and study the minimal singlet-doublet realization, denoted T4-3-i-B1, which contains one Dirac fermion, one Majorana fermion, an inert scalar doublet, and an inert scalar singlet. The resulting rank-two neutrino mass matrix predicts one massless neutrino. We confront both normal and inverted neutrino-mass orderings with neutrino-oscillation and cosmological data, charged-lepton flavor violation, including $μ-e$ conversion, electroweak precision observables, $h\toγγ$, theoretical consistency conditions, the relic abundance, and direct-detection limits. Both fermionic and scalar dark matter are viable. The fermionic candidate has only a loop-induced Higgs coupling and consequently a strongly suppressed spin-independent scattering rate, whereas the scalar candidate couples through a tree-level Higgs portal and can lie above the neutrino floor while remaining compatible with current limits. In both cases, coannihilation with inert scalars is essential for reproducing the observed relic abundance.

</details>


### [144] [Massive cold hybrid stars in a modified Polyakov-Nambu-Jona-Lasinio model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12653)
*Sk Md Adil Imam,Pedro Costa,Mariana Dutra,Odilon Lourenço,Renan Pereira,Constança Providência*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个修正的Polyakov-loop Nambu--Jona-Lasinio (mPNJL)模型，其中Polyakov势显式依赖于夸克化学势，使其在零温下保持有限，从而能够描述冷致密物质中的禁闭-退禁闭相变。通过Maxwell构造将该夸克部分与强子状态方程结合，发现根据模型参数不同，状态方程可呈现强子物质→禁闭（夸克子）夸克物质→退禁闭夸克物质的两步相变，或直接从强子到退禁闭/夸克子夸克物质的单步相变。研究系统性地分析了Polyakov势参数和矢量相互作用如何控制相变位置，发现排斥性矢量相互作用对获得稳定夸克核至关重要。具有夸克子和/或退禁闭核的混合星最大质量可超过2倍太阳质量，且在最大质量星核中，夸克子核星的声音速度超过共形极限1/3。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准PNJL模型在零温、高化学势条件下Polyakov势发散，无法描述冷致密物质中的禁闭-退禁闭相变。本文旨在通过将Polyakov势显式耦合化学势来修正该缺陷，从而研究冷致密夸克物质与混合星结构。

Method: 构造化学势依赖的修正Polyakov势的mPNJL模型，使之在T=0时保持有限；通过Maxwell构建将夸克部分状态方程与强子状态方程相匹配，形成混合星状态方程；系统扫描Polyakov势参数与夸克矢量耦合参数，分析相变结构及混合星的M-R关系和声速行为。

Result: 模型可实现两种相变路径（强子→夸克子物质→退禁闭夸克物质）或单步直接相变；排斥性矢量相互作用是获得稳定夸克核的必要条件；采用足够硬的强子状态方程时，混合星最大质量可超过2倍太阳质量；最大质量夸克子核星核内声速平方超过共形极限1/3。

Conclusion: 该工作系统地阐明了每个模型参数在塑造混合星结构中的定性作用，为冷致密夸克物质的相结构和致密星性质提供了新的物理图像，并预测了夸克子相及超共形声速等可观测检验的特征。

Abstract: We propose a modified Polyakov-loop Nambu--Jona-Lasinio (mPNJL) model in which the Polyakov potential is given by an explicit dependence on the quark chemical potential, allowing it to remain finite at zero temperature and thus to describe the confinement-deconfinement transition in cold dense matter. Combining this modified quark sector with hadronic equations of state via a Maxwell construction, we find that, depending on the model parameters, the equation of state can exhibit either two phase transitions, from hadronic matter to confined (quarkyonic) quark matter and subsequently to deconfined quark matter, or a single transition directly from hadronic to deconfined quark matter or from hadronic to quarkyonic quark matter. Stable massive cold hybrid stars with only quarkyonic and/or deconfined quark phase are obtained. We systematically examine how the parameters of the modified Polyakov potential and the quark vector interactions control the location of these transitions, and find that repulsive vector interactions are essential to obtain a stable quark core. Hybrid stars with quarkyonic and/or a deconfined core can reach maximum masses above $2M_\odot$, provided a sufficiently stiff hadronic equation of state is used at low density. In the core of the maximum-mass configurations, the speed of sound exceeds the conformal limit, $c_s^2 = 1/3$, for the quarkyonic core stars. This work establishes the qualitative role of each model parameter in shaping hybrid-star structure.

</details>


### [145] [Scalar induced gravitational waves as probes of dark QCD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12706)
*Wan-Zhe Feng,Ao Li,Jing-Zhi Zhou*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了标量诱导引力波（SIGWs）作为探测暗QCD交叉相变的探针。作者考虑了一个禁闭尺度约为标准模型QCD尺度5.5倍的暗QCD扇区，构建了包含暗QCD扇区的有效能量和熵自由度，求解了标量一阶扰动和二阶SIGW方程，发现暗QCD交叉相变会在增强标量模重新进入视界时产生频率移位的额外畸变，可作为暗QCD扇区的特征信号，为隐藏禁闭扇区提供互补的宇宙学探针。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受孪生希格斯和不对称孪生重子暗物质模型启发，需要寻找探测隐藏禁闭扇区（特别是暗QCD）的宇宙学方法。SIGWs作为尺度不变的引力波信号，有望在热历史上留下禁闭相变的特征印迹，从而为暗QCD扇区提供新的观测途径。

Method: 作者为包含三种或六种轻暗夸克味的暗QCD扇区构建有效能量/熵自由度，计算状态方程参数和声速；然后求解SM与暗QCD交叉时期的标量一阶扰动和二阶SIGW；对于单色原初曲率功率谱，比较理想化辐射主导与真实SM热历史情况，并分析暗QCD交叉对SIGW谱的影响。

Result: 结果表明：真实SM热历史会相对于理想辐射主导情形改变SIGW谱；暗QCD交叉相变在增强标量模于暗禁闭尺度附近重新进入视界时，产生一个频率移位的额外畸变特征；该特征相对SM QCD印记向更高频率移动。

Conclusion: SIGWs为隐藏禁闭扇区提供了互补的宇宙学探针，暗QCD交叉产生的频率移位畸变可作为暗QCD扇区的特征签名。该分析方法可推广到其他含有不同暗禁闭扇区的理论模型。

Abstract: We investigate scalar induced gravitational waves (SIGWs) as probes of a dark QCD crossover. Motivated by twin Higgs and asymmetric twin baryon dark matter scenarios, we consider a dark QCD sector with a confinement scale approximately 5.5 times the Standard Model (SM) QCD scale. We construct the effective energy and entropy degrees of freedom for the SM supplemented by dark QCD sectors containing either three light dark quark flavors or all six dark quark flavors. The resulting equation of state parameter and sound speed are then used to solve the first-order scalar perturbations and the second-order SIGWs through the SM and dark QCD crossover epochs. For a monochromatic primordial curvature power spectrum, we first demonstrate that the realistic SM thermal history modifies the SIGW spectrum relative to the idealized radiation-dominated case. We then show that a dark QCD crossover generates an additional frequency-shifted distortion when the enhanced scalar mode reenters the horizon near the dark confinement scale. This distinctive feature can therefore serve as a characteristic signature of the dark QCD sector. Our results demonstrate that SIGWs provide a complementary cosmological probe of hidden confining sectors, with characteristic spectral features shifted to higher frequencies relative to the SM QCD imprint. The analysis developed in this work can also be extended to other well-motivated theories containing different dark confining sectors.

</details>


### [146] [Topological diagrams of $Ω^0_c$ decays in the $SU(3)_F$ limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12742)
*Ying-Xin Lai,Di Wang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在SU(3)_F极限下系统研究了Ω_c^0重子弱衰变的拓扑振幅，完整给出了其衰变到十重态和八重态重子的树图和企鹅图贡献，并通过张量分析推导出拓扑振幅与SU(3)不可约振幅之间的线性关系，同时获得了多个同位旋关系及可用于检验Körner-Pati-Woo定理的关系式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ω_c^0重子是独特的粲味六重态重子，仅通过弱相互作用衰变。研究其衰变振幅结构有助于理解重子弱衰变机制，尤其是SU(3)_F对称性在粲重子衰变中的应用，以及对Körner-Pati-Woo定理等理论预言的检验。

Method: 在SU(3)_F极限下分析Ω_c^0衰变的拓扑振幅，完整列出其衰变到十重态和八重态重子的树图和企鹅图贡献，通过张量分析推导拓扑振幅与SU(3)不可约振幅之间的线性关系，并利用同位旋对称性推导相关关系式。

Result: 完整给出了Ω_c^0衰变到十重态和八重态重子的树图与企鹅图拓扑振幅，推导出拓扑振幅与SU(3)不可约振幅间的线性关系，获得多个同位旋关系，并导出了可用于检验Körner-Pati-Woo定理的关系式。

Conclusion: 该工作为Ω_c^0衰变提供了完整的振幅框架，所得同位旋关系和Körner-Pati-Woo定理检验关系可用于后续实验测量，从而检验SU(3)_F对称性和标准模型预言。

Abstract: The $Ω^0_c$ baryon is a unique charmed sextet baryon as it decays through weak interaction. In this work, we investigate the topological amplitudes of $Ω_c^0$ decays in the $SU(3)_F$ limit. The tree- and penguin-induced diagrams contributing to $Ω_c^0$ decays into decuplet and octet baryons are presented completely. The linear relations between the topological amplitudes and the $SU(3)$ irreducible amplitudes are derived via tensor analysis. Several isospin relations are obtained, and some relations are derived to test the Körner-Pati-Woo theorem.

</details>


### [147] [In-medium properties of $D$ and $D^*$ mesons in magnetized isospin asymmetric nuclear matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12834)
*Saksham Sharma,Dhananjay Singh,Suneel Dutt,Harleen Dahiya,Arvind Kumar*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在等自旋不对称核物质中，结合手征SU(3)夸克平均场模型(CQMF)和光前夸克模型(LFQM)，系统研究了外部磁场对赝标量和矢量D介子性质的影响，发现磁场通过磁催化增强介子质量和衰变常数，而重子密度产生吸引质量偏移并抑制衰变常数和分布振幅，朗道量子化进一步增强带电介子质量，赝标量-矢量混合则产生能级排斥，最终为FAIR、NICA和J-PARC等重味物理实验提供了理论洞见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解强相互作用物质中重味介子在极端条件下（强磁场、高温、高密度）的行为，对解释重离子碰撞实验中的重味动力学至关重要，并可为FAIR、NICA和J-PARC等实验设施提供理论预测。

Method: 采用混合理论框架：将CQMF模型（包含磁化狄拉克海贡献和核子反常磁矩）计算得到的介质修正夸克质量作为LFQM的输入，计算介子质量、弱衰变常数和前导扭曲分布振幅；同时纳入带电介子的朗道量子化（限制在最低朗道能级）以及磁场诱导的赝标量-矢量混合。

Result: 外部磁场通过磁催化增强赝标量和矢量D介子的有效质量和衰变常数；重子密度增大导致吸引质量偏移并抑制衰变常数和分布振幅；朗道能级贡献进一步增强带电介子质量；赝标量-矢量混合产生能级排斥（矢量介子上移、赝标量介子下移）；等自旋不对称在所有介子态中诱导微小但系统的质量劈裂。

Conclusion: 磁场与密度效应的相互作用产生了重轻介子性质的非平凡介质行为，这些结果对强相互作用物质中的重味动力学提供了有价值的见解，可应用于FAIR、NICA和J-PARC的当前及未来研究。

Abstract: We investigate the impact of an external magnetic field on the in-medium properties of pseudoscalar ($D^0,D^+,D_s$) and vector ($D^{0*},D^{+*},D_{s}^{*}$) mesons in isospin asymmetric nuclear matter at finite temperature using a hybrid theoretical framework combining the chiral SU(3) quark mean-field (CQMF) model and the light-front quark model (LFQM). The medium-modified constituent quark masses, obtained from the CQMF model by including the magnetized Dirac sea contribution and anomalous magnetic moments of nucleons, are used as input to the LFQM calculations of meson masses, weak decay constants, and leading-twist distribution amplitudes. We further incorporate the Landau quantization of the charged mesons restricted to the lowest Landau level, while magnetic field induced pseudoscalar-vector mixing is taken into account for each $D$-$D^{*}$ doublet. We find that the external magnetic field enhances the effective masses and decay constants of both pseudoscalar and vector $D$ mesons by magnetic catalysis, while increasing baryon density generally induces an attractive mass shift and suppresses the decay constant and distribution amplitudes. The Landau level contribution further enhances the effective masses of the charged mesons, whereas the pseudoscalar-vector mixing produces a level repulsion, shifting the vector meson masses upward and the pseudoscalar meson masses downward. The interplay between magnetic field and density effects gives rise to a nontrivial medium behavior of heavy-light meson properties, with isospin asymmetry further inducing a small but systematic mass splitting across all the meson states considered. These results provide useful insights into heavy-flavor dynamics in strongly interacting matter and are relevant to ongoing and future studies at FAIR, NICA, and J-PARC.

</details>


### [148] [Entropy from inclusive scattering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12923)
*M. A. Braun*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究在无Pomeron间相互作用的幺正Pomeron交换模型中，从多个非弹性截面引入概率的问题。研究发现按此方式得到的非弹性截面与幺正性要求的截面存在偏差，该偏差随能量增加而减小并在高能极限下消失。数值计算给出的概率和熵显示：与朴素预测相反，熵随能量先上升，在快度12-17处达到最大值后随能量升高缓慢下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者试图在简化的幺正Pomeron模型（忽略Pomeron间相互作用）中，检验由多个非弹性截面推导出的概率分布是否满足幺正性要求，以及由此得到的熵随能量的变化行为是否与直觉预测一致。

Method: 采用无Pomeron间相互作用的幺正Pomeron交换模型，从多个非弹性截面中提取概率分布，并将所得非弹性截面与幺正性要求进行比较；对概率和熵进行数值计算，分析其随能量（快度）的变化规律。

Result: 发现由非弹性截面得到的概率分布与幺正性之间存在系统性偏差，该偏差随能量升高而衰减，在高能极限下消失。熵随能量的变化呈非单调行为：先上升，在快度12-17处达到峰值，之后随能量增高缓慢下降。

Conclusion: 在高能极限下，从多个非弹性截面获得的概率分布能够自洽地满足幺正性要求；但熵的非单调能量依赖表明体系在有限能量下存在显著的动力学修正，单纯依赖与能量无关Pomeron交换的模型无法完整描述中等能量区域的散射特征。

Abstract: Introduction of probabilities from multiple inclusive cross-sections is studied
  in the unitary pomeron exchange models with no interaction between the pomerons themselves.
  Discrepancy is observed between the emerging inelasic cross-section and the one following from unitarity.
  It diminishes with energy and disappears in the high-energy limit. Probabilities and
  entropy are calculated numerically. Contrary to naive predictions as a function of energy the entropy first rises and after achievung its maximum at rapidity 12-17 slowly falls for higher energies

</details>


### [149] [Newtrinos.jl: A Julia Package for Global Analysis of Neutrino Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12940)
*Philipp Eller,David Schultheiß*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: Newtrinos.jl是一个开源的Julia软件包，用于中微子数据的全局分析，采用模块化三层架构（物理模型、实验描述、统计推断），支持频繁派和贝叶斯推断，可组合联合似然，并支持自动微分和并行计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中微子数据的全局分析需要灵活的工具，能够组合不同实验和理论模型，同时处理系统不确定性并进行现代统计推断，因此需要一个模块化、可组合、可扩展的分析框架。

Method: 采用三层模块化架构，分离物理模型、实验描述和统计推断；为每个实验数据集实现完整的统计前向模型（包括似然函数和数据生成过程）；使用Julia编写，实现全模型自动微分和精确梯度计算；支持CPU线程或分布式并行。

Result: 该框架支持实验与物理模型的自由组合、无需修改核心代码即可添加新实验和模型、可构建多数据集的联合似然、支持跨实验的参数合并/相关/去相关、兼容Frequentist和Bayesian推理，且完全可并行化。

Conclusion: Newtrinos.jl提供了一个高度可组合、可扩展且统计上严谨的中微子全局分析框架，代表了该领域开源工具的现代化发展方向。

Abstract: Newtrinos.jl is an open-source Julia package for performing global analyses of neutrino data. It provides a modular, three-layer architecture that separates physics models, experiment descriptions, and statistical inference into independent modules. This allows researchers to freely combine experiments and test them against a variety of theoretical models. New experiments and physics models can be added without modifying core code. Full statistical forward models including all relevant systematic uncertainties are implemented for each experimental dataset, defining both the likelihood and the data-generating process and enabling modern statistical inference workflows. The framework is composable, making it straightforward to construct joint likelihoods over multiple datasets. Physics and nuisance parameters can be merged, correlated, or decorrelated across experiments to ensure consistency in the joint fit. The package supports Frequentist and Bayesian inference and is parallelizable across CPU threads or distributed workers. Written entirely in Julia, all models are automatically differentiable, enabling exact gradient computation.

</details>


### [150] [Decay widths of radially excited vector mesons of the $U(3)$ nonet with production of three pseudoscalar mesons in the extended NJL model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13140)
*M. K. Volkov,A. A. Pivovarov,K. Nurlan*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 在扩展NJL模型中计算了第一径向激发矢量介子ρ', ω', φ'和K*'产生3P（P=K,π,η）的衰变过程，考虑箱图和含基态及第一径向激发态的中间矢量介子通道，结果可作为未来实验的预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对第一径向激发矢量介子的多介子衰变过程进行理论研究，为未来实验提供可验证的预测。

Method: 使用扩展NJL模型，计算第一径向激发矢量介子（ρ', ω', φ', K*'）衰变到3个赝标量介子（K, π, η）的过程，考虑了箱图（box channels）通道和包含基态及第一径向激发态中间矢量介子的贡献通道。

Result: 计算得到了这些衰变过程的具体结果，可作为未来实验的预测依据。

Conclusion: 该研究为第一径向激发矢量介子衰变到三赝标量介子的过程提供了理论预测，有待未来实验验证。

Abstract: In the extended NJL model, the decays of the first radially excited vector mesons $ρ'$,$ω'$, $φ'$ and ${K^*}'$ with production of $3P$, where $P=K,π,η$ are calculated. The box channels and the channels with intermediate vector mesons in the ground and first radially excited states are considered. The obtained results can be considered as predictions for future experiments.

</details>


### [151] [Magnetization and Magnetic Field-Induced Correction: Implications for QGP Thermal Photon Production in Magnetohydrodynamic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13364)
*Jing Jing,Duan She,Ze-Fang Jiang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了磁化夸克-胶子等离子体（QGP）中的热光子发射，在(1+1)维相对论磁流体动力学（MHD）框架下，系统考虑了磁化率χ_m（包括常数和格点QCD温度依赖参数化）以及弱场量子修正费米子分布函数f_EM。发现光子产额主要由初始磁场强度及其时间衰减轮廓主导，χ_m影响可忽略，而弱场修正f_EM在中横动量区间引起显著的光子产生增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究磁化QGP中的电磁信号，特别是热光子发射，以深入理解强磁场下夸克胶子等离子体的性质和磁效应。

Method: 采用Pu-Bjorken MHD框架，计算Compton散射、q̄q湮灭、韧致辐射和再散射湮灭的光子产生率，并在QGP时空演化上积分得到横动量谱。

Result: 光子产额主要由初始磁场强度和磁场时间衰减轮廓控制，χ_m在所探索的参数空间中影响可忽略；弱场修正f_EM在中横动量（p_T）区域诱导出明显的热光子产生增强效应。

Conclusion: 为磁化QGP中电磁可观测量建立了严格的理论框架，并为未来包含自旋-磁化动力学的耗散MHD研究奠定基础。

Abstract: We investigate thermal photon emission from magnetized quark-gluon plasma (QGP) within (1+1)-dimensional relativistic magnetohydrodynamics (MHD), systematically incorporating magnetic susceptibility $χ_m$---encompassing both constant and lattice-QCD-derived temperature-dependent $χ_m(T)$ parametrizations---and weak-field quantum corrections to quark distribution functions $f_{\rm EM}$. Employing the Pu-Bjorken MHD framework, we calculate photon production rates from Compton scattering, $q\bar{q}$ annihilation, bremsstrahlung, and annihilation with rescattering, and integrate these over the QGP spacetime evolution to obtain transverse momentum ($p_T$) spectra. Our results demonstrate that photon yields are predominantly governed by the initial magnetic field strength and its temporal decay profile, with $χ_m$ exerting negligible influence in the explored parameter space. In contrast, the weak-field correction $f_{\rm EM}$ induces a distinct enhancement in thermal photon production at intermediate $p_T$. This work establishes a rigorous theoretical framework for quantifying electromagnetic observables in magnetized QGP and provides the foundation for future dissipative MHD studies incorporating spin-magnetization dynamics.

</details>


### [152] [Non-perturbative quark production and transport in the evolving glasma: the WAGASHI event generator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13393)
*Nicholas J. Benoit,Chiho Nonaka,Hidetoshi Taya*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文发展了一个新模型WAGASHI，将经典杨-米尔斯玻璃态演化、Wong方程输运和Schwinger对产生结合到逐事件的基础上，研究相对论重离子碰撞早期glasma阶段的非微扰夸克-反夸克对产生及后续夸克动力学。研究发现大量夸克（与最终强子产额可比）在glasma阶段就已产生，并经历显著的动量展宽和自旋随机化，表明其对夸克-胶子等离子体早期热化有重要贡献。该模型还提供了重离子碰撞后流体动力学演化的动力学初始条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解相对论重离子碰撞中最早期glasma阶段的非微扰夸克对产生及其对夸克-胶子等离子体（QGP）早期热化的贡献，并为后续流体动力学演化提供动态初始条件。

Method: 开发WAGASHI模型，在逐事件基础上结合三个关键组成部分：（1）经典杨-米尔斯（CYM）glasma演化；（2）Wong方程输运描述夸克在色场中的运动；（3）Schwinger机制描述非微扰夸克-反夸克对产生。

Result: 在glasma阶段已经产生了与最终强子产额相当的大量夸克，这些夸克经历了显著的动量展宽和自旋随机化。确定了Pb-Pb和O-O碰撞中重子数、电荷、奇异性和自旋极化的逐事件分布，发现较小的O-O体系中涨落特别大。

Conclusion: glasma阶段的夸克产生和动力学过程对QGP的早期热化有显著贡献，WAGASHI模型提供的结果可作为后续QGP流体动力学演化的动力学初始条件。

Abstract: We study non-perturbative quark-antiquark pair production and the subsequent quark dynamics in the earliest glasma stage of relativistic heavy-ion collisions. For that, we develop a new model, WAGASHI (Wong-precessing Anisotropic Glasma And ScHwinger-produced Initial-conditions), which combines classical Yang-Mills glasma evolution, Wong-equation transport, and Schwinger pair production on an event-by-event basis. We find that a sizeable number of quarks, comparable to the final hadron yields, are produced already during the glasma stage and subsequently undergo substantial momentum broadening and spin randomization, suggesting a significant contribution toward the early equilibration of the quark-gluon plasma. We also determine the event-by-event distributions of baryon number, electric charge, strangeness, and spin polarization in Pb-Pb and O-O collisions at LHC energies, finding particularly large fluctuations in the smaller O-O system. These results provide dynamical initial conditions for the subsequent hydrodynamic evolution of the quark-gluon plasma.

</details>


### [153] [Critical behavior and critical exponents of rotating QCD matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13469)
*Kai Xiao,Fei Sun,Shuang Li,Xun Chen*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在平均场近似下，利用两味NJL模型研究了旋转强相互作用物质的热力学性质与临界行为。通过温度-角速度平面确定相结构与临界端点（CEP），分析热力学可观测量在CEP附近的奇异行为，提取有效临界指数，并验证其满足标度关系，表明旋转自由度不改变平均场临界标度行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旋转QCD物质（如重离子碰撞中的大角动量系统）是强相互作用物质研究的重要方向，但旋转效应对相结构、临界端点及临界标度行为的影响尚缺乏系统性理解。本文旨在通过NJL模型在平均场框架下系统表征旋转诱导的临界现象。

Method: 采用两味NJL模型，在平均场近似下研究旋转强相互作用物质。在温度-角速度（T,ω）平面上确定相结构和CEP。通过分析热力学可观测量（比热密度、旋转极化不连续量、旋转磁化率、旋转极化临界等温线）在CEP附近的奇异行为，提取有效临界指数并检验标度关系。

Result: 提取的临界指数趋近于预期的平均场值，并满足相应的标度关系。结果表明旋转自由度在平均场框架内不改变底层临界标度行为。

Conclusion: 该系统研究为旋转诱导的临界现象提供了系统性表征，并为超越平均场近似研究旋转QCD物质奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: We investigate the thermodynamic properties and critical behavior of rotating strongly interacting matter within the two-flavor Nambu--Jona-Lasinio (NJL) model in the mean-field approximation. The phase structure and the critical endpoint (CEP) are determined in the temperature--angular velocity \((T,ω)\) plane. By analyzing the singular behavior of thermodynamic observables near the CEP, we extract the corresponding effective critical exponents characterizing the scaling behavior of the specific heat density, the rotational polarization discontinuity, the rotational susceptibility, and the critical-isotherm behavior of the rotational polarization. The obtained exponents approach the expected mean-field values and satisfy the corresponding scaling relations, indicating that the rotational degree of freedom does not alter the underlying mean-field critical scaling behavior within the present framework. These results provide a systematic characterization of rotation-induced critical phenomena and establish a basis for further studies of rotating QCD matter beyond the mean-field approximation.

</details>


### [154] [A pion-driven near-threshold enhancement in the $π\,T_{cc}$ system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13525)
*Pedro Brandão,Jorgivan Morais Dias,Luciano M. Abreu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过Faddeev方程的固定中心近似，研究π介子散射于T_cc^+(3875)分子态，发现在πT_cc阈值附近约4055 MeV处出现一个明显的三体共振增强，宽度约60 MeV，表明已知的强子分子态可作为构建更复杂奇特强子的积木，并建议在T_ccπ不变质量谱（特别是D^0D^0π^+π^-末态）中寻找这一信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强子分子态通常被视为近阈两体动力学产物，作者希望探索一个实验上已确认的分子态能否作为构建更复杂奇特强子的基本单元，从而验证三体动力学能否产生更重的近阈结构。

Method: 将T_cc视为同位旋标量的DD*关联分子簇，在I(J^P)=1(1^-)道中，利用Faddeev方程的固定中心近似描述πDD*三体动力学，求解完整的三体散射振幅。

Result: 三体振幅在πT_cc阈值稍上方出现显著增强，峰位置约为4055 MeV，表观宽度约60 MeV；振幅实部和虚部的行为与共振解释相容。

Conclusion: 结果表明最轻强子（π）能够在动力学上发挥积极作用，将已观测到的强子分子态促进为更重近阈结构的构建块，该结构由三体动力学产生；建议在T_ccπ不变质量谱中（尤其D^0D^0π^+π^-末态）寻找此增强信号。

Abstract: Hadronic molecules are commonly viewed as products of near-threshold two-body dynamics. We investigate whether an experimentally established molecule can also serve as a building block for a more complex exotic hadron by studying pion scattering off $T^+_{cc}(3875)$. Treating $T_{cc}$ as a correlated isoscalar $D D^*$ molecular cluster, we describe the resulting $πD D^*$ dynamics in the $I(J^P)=1(1^-)$ channel within the Fixed Center Approximation to the Faddeev equations. The full three-body amplitude develops a pronounced enhancement slightly above the $πT_{cc}$ threshold, centered at $M_{\rm peak}\simeq 4055$~MeV, with an apparent width of approximately $60$~MeV. The associated behavior of the real and imaginary parts of the amplitude is compatible with a resonant-like interpretation. These results show that the lightest hadron can play an active dynamical role in promoting an observed hadronic molecule into a building block of a heavier near-threshold structure generated by the three-body dynamics. We propose searching for this enhancement in the $T_{cc}π$ invariant-mass distribution, particularly through the $D^0D^0π^+π^-$ final state.

</details>


### [155] [Kinematic Fingerprints of a Nucleon-Triggered $V_{\mathcal{B}}$ in Rare $η^{(\prime)}\toπ^0(η)γγ$ Decays on Nucleon Targets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13537)
*Yaroslav Balytskyi*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究由核子触发的矢量玻色子模型V_B，该模型旨在解释KLOE与MAMI在η→π⁰γγ衰变分支比测量之间的显著差异。文章量化了MAMI与JEF两个生产能量区域之间的残余运动学与截断依赖效应，指出模型最干净的判别特征是γγ和π⁰γ谱中的运动学禁戒尾部（在1200样本中预测4-23个事件），并识别了可能贡献于γd→π⁰ηd和γd→π⁰π⁰d角分布差异的V_B诱导拓扑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KLOE对η→π⁰γγ分支比的测量结果(0.98±0.11±0.14)×10⁻⁴与由MAMI光致产生数据主导的世界平均值(2.55±0.22)×10⁻⁴之间存在统计学显著差异。核子触发矢量玻色子模型V_B被提出以解释这一差异，因其仅由外部核子激活，不影响轻子测量（如KLOE和BESIII），故需要系统研究该模型的可观测效应及其判别特征。

Method: 论文量化了V_B模型在MAMI与JEF两种不同生产能量区域下的残余运动学和截断依赖效应；通过运动学禁戒尾部（γγ和π⁰γ谱）作为模型判别器，计算了不同选择窗口下的事件率预测；比较了两种能量区域的类有效分支比差异、平均重建质量位移，并识别了V_B诱导的拓扑结构以解释γd→π⁰ηd和γd→π⁰π⁰d角分布的差异。

Result: 在1200事件样本中，运动学禁戒尾部预测4-23个事件（取决于选择窗口）。反冲质子极角相对在壳假设可达约-5°（MAMI能量）或仅约-0.2°（JEF）。相同选择窗口下，JEF类有效分支比超出MAMI类预测不到1%，但加宽窗口可使两者预测增加约4%。两个能量区域对平均重建质量⟨M_{π⁰γγ}⟩-m_η产生类似的2-7 MeV向上位移。

Conclusion: V_B模型在MAMI和JEF能量下产生接近但不完全相同的预测，运动学禁戒尾部是模型最干净的实验探针。该模型还可能为γd→π⁰ηd和γd→π⁰π⁰d中已报道的角分布差异提供潜在解释，但定量研究留待未来工作。

Abstract: The nucleon-triggered vector-boson scenario $V_{\mathcal B}$ was motivated by a statistically significant discrepancy between the KLOE measurement, $\mathrm{BR}^{η\rightarrowπ^{0}γγ}_{\text{KLOE}} = \left(0.98\pm0.11_{\text{stat}}\pm0.14_{\text{syst}}\right)\times10^{-4}$, and the current world average, $\left(2.55\pm0.22\right)\times10^{-4}$, dominated by MAMI photoproduction data. In this model, the new interaction is activated by external nucleons, which causes deviations from the Standard Model predictions, while leaving leptonic measurements, such as KLOE and BESIII, unaffected. We quantify the residual kinematic and cut-dependent effects that make the MAMI- and JEF-like predictions close but not identical due to their different production-energy regimes. The cleanest discriminator of the model is a kinematically forbidden tail in the $γγ$ and $π^0γ$ spectra, and for $η\rightarrowπ^0γγ$, and we predict 4--23 events in a sample of 1200, depending on the selection window, in these regions. These events correlate with the kinematics of the recoil proton, and the polar angle relative to the on-shell hypothesis can reach $\approx-5^\circ$ or below at MAMI energies, but only $\approx -0.2^\circ$ at JEF. For identical selection windows, the JEF-like effective branching fraction exceeds the MAMI-like prediction by less than $1\%$, while widening the window increases both predictions by up to $\sim4\%$. Both energy regimes produce a similar upward shift of the mean reconstructed mass, $\langle M_{π^0γγ}\rangle-m_η\simeq 2$--$7~\mathrm{MeV}$. Finally, we identify representative $V_{\mathcal B}$-induced topologies that could contribute to recently reported discrepancies in the angular distributions of $γd\toπ^0ηd$ and $γd\toπ^0π^0 d$, leaving their quantitative investigation to future work.

</details>
